By mining specification-permitted or implementation-tolerated representation gaps at the PDF render/extract boundary, we instantiate 25 extraction gaps (EG) in which extractors return attacker-controlled or extractor-dependent text while the rendered page shows benign or different content. The gaps form four families: semantic overrides, hidden semantic injection, reading-order splits, and font-decoding splits, and 14 gaps have no exact path/mechanism-level match in prior PDF-to-LLM attacks.
  We evaluate these gaps on 16 PDF processing stacks and 7 commercial LLM services. Each gap causes render-extract divergence on at least one stack. Under a gap-level exposure criterion, every evaluated service exposes at least one gap, with 12/25 to 21/25 exposed gaps. Exposure is driven mainly by the ingestion stack -- not model identity alone. We further show that tested safety filters cover only selected hidden-text constructions. To support triage, we develop a static screening scanner whose rules trigger on all 25 benchmark gaps, and discuss dual-view consistency as a longer-term defense direction.

</details>


### [147] [BT-MTD: Bus Traversal-based Moving Target Defense for Smart Grid](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15047)
*Jingyi Yan,Ke Sun,Zhenglin Li,Hongying Jia*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于母线遍历的移动目标防御（BT-MTD）策略，通过识别并避免无效支路导纳修改，以更少的支路修改次数和更短的计算时间有效提升电网的网络安全弹性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的MTD策略在修改支路导纳时存在大量无效修改，不仅增加了计算负担，也无法有效提升防御效果。作者旨在通过理论分析识别这些无效支路，从而在保证防御效果的前提下，减少修改次数和计算时间。

Method: 首先通过理论分析识别出导纳修改对MTD有效性无贡献的"无效支路"；在此基础上，提出BT-MTD算法，该算法基于母线遍历方式，依据解析推导的准则在网络母线上进行遍历搜索，选择有效的支路进行导纳修改。

Result: 在标准IEEE测试系统上与四种现有策略进行对比，BT-MTD在防御有效性、运行效率以及计算开销方面均表现出更优的性能。

Conclusion: BT-MTD能够以更少的支路修改次数和更短的计算时间实现有效的电网移动目标防御，是一种鲁棒性强、性能优越的MTD策略。

Abstract: Moving Target Defense (MTD) is a proactive security strategy designed to enhance cyber-resilience by dynamically altering system parameters, thereby preventing adversaries from acquiring the critical information needed to execute stealth attacks. In this paper, we consider the case in which the operator modifies the admittance of branches to enable MTD, and focus on the problem of effectively protecting the system with fewer number of branch admittance modifications and shorter computational time. Specifically, we identify the ineffectual branches whose admittance modification do not contribute to the improvement of MTD effectiveness via theoretical analysis. Building on these insights, we propose the Bus Traversal-based MTD (BT-MTD), which is a bus-oriented algorithm that traverses over the buses of the network according to analytically derived guidelines. The performance of the BT-MTD is evaluated and compared with four existing strategies on standard IEEE test systems, demonstrating its robustness and superior performance in effectiveness, efficiency, and computational cost. The code of BT-MTD is available at: https://github.com/YJY101/BT-MTD.

</details>


### [148] [AutoDojo: Adaptive Attacks Expose Superficial Defenses and User-Underspecification Limits in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15057)
*Xinhang Ma,Taoran Li,Chaowei Xiao,Zhiyuan Yu,Ning Zhang,Yevgeniy Vorobeychik*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出AutoDojo，一种针对LLM代理的间接提示注入（IPI）防御的自适应评估框架。通过使用前沿LLM迭代优化注入，AutoDojo能大幅提升攻击成功率，揭示现有防御在自适应威胁下的脆弱性，尤其是在"动作开放"任务中防御存在结构性局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的IPI防御评估基准（如AgentDojo）本质上是静态的，只能生成固定分布的IPI攻击，无法有效评估防御对自适应威胁的鲁棒性。因此需要一种能够自适应优化攻击的评估框架来真实衡量防御效果。

Method: 开发AutoDojo作为AgentDojo的自适应扩展，使用前沿LLM作为攻击者，通过黑盒方式迭代优化注入文本，针对给定防御策略生成更有效的IPI攻击。在三个任务套件和五个目标模型上对多种SOTA防御进行评估。

Result: 1) 大多数防御仅提供有限保护：AutoDojo的自适应攻击将攻击成功率（ASR）显著提升至远超静态注入的水平，即使是将静态ASR降至0%的过滤器，AutoDojo也能恢复28%的整体ASR（动作开放任务上达64%）。2) 在"动作开放"任务中，ASR远高于精确指定任务，因为注入可伪装为普通数据而非显式指令，绕过依赖检测指令文本的防御。

Conclusion: 现有的提示级和过滤器级防御在自适应攻击下存在结构性局限，尤其是在动作开放任务中。AutoDojo为评估IPI防御的鲁棒性提供了更真实的基准，揭示了防御设计的改进方向。

Abstract: Indirect prompt injection (IPI) is a major security threat to LLM-powered agents. Thus, a growing body of work have proposed a variety of defensive approaches against IPI. These can be grouped into three broad categories: 1) prompt-based (using prompting as a way to prevent agents from following malicious instructions), 2) detection-based (identifying and filtering malicious instructions), and 3) system-level (using systems insights, such as control and data isolation, for defense). However, commonly used benchmarks for evaluating defense, such as AgentDojo, are \emph{inherently static}, generating a fixed distribution of IPI attacks. Consequently, static benchmarks do not usefully evaluate defense robustness to adaptive threats. We address this issue by developing AutoDojo, an adaptive extension of AgentDojo that optimizes IPI against a given defense. Using AutoDojo against state-of-the-art IPI defenses across three task suites and five target models, we make two key observations. First, many defenses offer only limited protection: a cheap, black-box adaptive attack using a frontier LLM to iteratively optimize the injection raises attack success rate (ASR) well above the level achieved by static injections against nearly all evaluated defenses. Against a filter that reduces static ASR to 0\%, AutoDojo recovers 28\% overall and 64\% on action-open tasks. Second, for prompt-level and filter-based defenses, ASR is substantially higher on \emph{action-open} tasks -- where the user's request delegates the action itself to attacker-controlled content -- than on precisely specified tasks. This is a structural limit: on such tasks the injection can pose as ordinary data rather than an explicit instruction, bypassing defenses that rely on detecting instruction-like text. AutoDojo is publicly available at https://github.com/xhOwenMa/AutoDojo.

</details>


### [149] [Fuzzy PSI from Symmetric Primitives with Exact Logarithmic Dependence on Distance Threshold](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15093)
*Cong Zhang,Yang Cao,Yujie Bai,Shuaishuai Li,Juntong Lin,Yu Chen,Anyu Wang,Xiaoyun Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套完全基于不经意传输(OT)和对称密钥原语构建的FPSI协议，实现了对L_{p∈[1,∞]}距离的对数复杂度，在运行时间和通信量上分别比现有最优方案提升最高43.7倍和31.3倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有FPSI方案要么仅支持L_∞距离并实现多对数复杂度，要么支持一般L_p距离但依赖昂贵的加性同态加密(AHE)。实现一般L_p距离下对阈值δ的严格对数依赖而不依赖AHE，是阈值缩放优化上的理论突破和可扩展FPSI应用的实际推进。

Method: 提出基于apart和separate两种假设的FPSI协议，分别适用于低维和高维场景。核心思想是通过前缀表示进行模糊匹配，并利用相等条件交互确定正确前缀。方案仅使用OT和对称密钥操作，避免了AHE开销。

Result: 实现了对δ的严格对数复杂度（理论最优，因为区分长度为O(δ)的区间内所有值至少需要Ω(log δ)比特信息）。实验表明，与现有最优方案相比，运行时间提升最高43.7倍，通信量提升最高31.3倍。

Conclusion: 该工作首次在不依赖AHE的情况下，对一般L_p距离实现了对数阈值缩放，兼具理论最优性和实际高效性，为大规模FPSI应用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Previous FPSI works have demonstrated a linear scaling with the distance threshold $δ$, while some recent works have achieved a poly-logarithmic dependence on $δ$. However, these protocols either support only the $L_\infty$ distance, or they support general $L_{p\in[1,\infty]}$ distances but rely on expensive additive homomorphic encryption (AHE). Achieving exact logarithmic dependence on $δ$ for general $L_{p\in[1,\infty]}$ distances without relying on costly AHE would constitute a theoretical breakthrough in optimal threshold scaling and a practical advance toward scalable FPSI applications.
  In this work, we present new FPSI protocols for $L_{p\in[1,\infty]}$ distances that are entirely built from oblivious transfer (OT) and symmetric-key primitives. We propose FPSI protocols based on both the apart and the separate assumptions, which are applicable to low- and high-dimensional settings, respectively. Our constructions achieve strictly logarithmic complexity in $δ$, which is optimal in the sense that distinguishing all values in an interval of length $O(δ)$ necessarily requires $Ω(\log δ)$ bits of information. Our core idea is to perform fuzzy matching via prefix representation and interactively determine the correct prefix using equality conditions. To this end, we propose a suite of new components that can be implemented efficiently using only OT and symmetric-key operations.
  We implement our FPSI protocols and compare them with the state-of-the-art FPSI protocols for $L_{p\in[1,\infty]}$ distance. Experiments show that our protocols outperform the prior state-of-the-art by up to $43.7\times$ in runtime and $31.3\times$ in communication.

</details>


### [150] [Data-Centric Benchmarking of Exploit Generation in LLMs: Understanding the Impact of Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15123)
*Yiwei Chen,Lichi Li,Kai Cheung,Vinny Parla,Ganesh Sundaram*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于CVE的漏洞利用生成任务，通过构建高质量数据集、提出LLM-as-judge评估框架，对17个大语言模型进行零样本能力评估，并证明8B参数的开源模型在微调后性能提升42.5%，接近闭源模型水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM在安全漏洞利用生成方面的能力缺乏系统评估，且缺乏高质量数据集和标准化评估方法，阻碍了该领域的发展。

Method: 采用数据驱动方法，通过多阶段预处理构建高质量数据集；提出可扩展的LLM-as-judge评估框架及细粒度评分标准；在统一设置下对17个LLM进行8项指标评估；对8B开源模型进行微调并采用简单测试时拒绝策略。

Result: 8B开源模型微调后利用质量提升42.5%，结合拒绝策略可媲美部分专有模型。

Conclusion: 数据质量、结构化监督和评估设计对于可靠的漏洞利用生成至关重要，这些因素与模型规模在适应网络安全任务中同等重要。

Abstract: We study the task of CVE-conditioned exploit generation, where a model drafts proof-of-concept (PoC) exploits given software vulnerability context. We adopt a data-centric approach, constructing a high-quality dataset via multi-stage preprocessing and introducing a scalable evaluation framework with LLM-as-judge and fine-grained rubrics. Under this unified setup, we benchmark 17 large language models across 8 evaluation criteria, providing systematic insights into their zero-shot capabilities. We further show that a compact 8B open-weight model, when fine-tuned on curated data, achieves over 42.5% improvement in exploit quality and rivals some proprietary models when combined with simple test-time rejection strategies. Our results highlight the importance of data quality, structured supervision, and evaluation design for reliable exploit generation, suggesting that these factors can be as critical as model scale in adapting LLMs to cybersecurity tasks.

</details>


### [151] [VLALeaks: Membership Inference Attacks against Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15165)
*Xukun Luan,Jinyan Liu,Xuesong Li,Yuanguo Bi,Renjun Wu,Zhongxiang Lei,Di Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次系统性地研究了VLA（视觉-语言-动作）模型中的成员推理攻击（MIA），提出VLALeaks方法，利用VLA模型的注意力差异，通过两阶段流程（成员特征提取+攻击模型构建）有效识别训练数据成员，揭示了VLA模型在隐私保护方面的严重漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型在实现端到端机器人控制的同时，由于训练数据记忆和机器人数据获取成本高昂，存在严重的数据隐私泄露和知识产权侵犯风险。成员推理攻击（MIA）在VLA模型中尚未被探索，亟需填补这一研究空白。

Method: 提出VLALeaks攻击方法，基于VLA模型中的注意力差异，包含两个阶段：(1) 成员特征提取——从VLA模型中提取与成员信息相关的特征； (2) 攻击模型构建——利用提取的特征训练攻击模型以判断样本是否属于训练集。

Result: 在多个VLA基准测试上的实验结果表明，VLALeaks能够有效揭示成员信息，在AUC和TPR@1%FPR指标上达到最优性能，证实了当前VLA模型部署中存在严重的隐私漏洞。

Conclusion: 这是首个针对VLA模型的MIA系统性研究，揭示了VLA模型的隐私脆弱性，为构建安全可信的VLA模型提供了重要启示。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models enable end-to-end robot control and have garnered widespread attention. However, the memorization of training data inherent to VLA, coupled with the high cost of robotic data acquisition, raises serious concerns regarding data privacy leakage and intellectual property infringement. Membership inference attacks (MIAs) aim to determine whether a given sample belongs to the training set. While representing a significant privacy threat, this attack remains underexplored in the context of VLA models. To bridge this gap, we propose VLALeaks, which is based on attention discrepancies in VLA models. We reveal, for the first time, the privacy vulnerabilities of VLA models. Specifically, it comprises a two-stage process: (1) membership feature extraction, and (2) attack model construction. Experimental results across multiple VLA benchmarks demonstrate that VLALeaks readily reveals membership information and achieves optimal attack AUC and TPR@1\%FPR, highlighting the privacy vulnerabilities in current VLA model deployments. Our work is the first systematic study of MIAs on VLA models, aiming to provide insights for secure and trustworthy VLA models.

</details>


### [152] [Robust and Precise Application Fingerprinting on 5G Physical Uplink Channel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15221)
*Yu Li,Liqi Zhuang,Dong Wei,Jiwen Luo,Hang Zhang,Meng Zhang,Xiaona Li,Weiqing Huang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文发现5G NR物理层侧信道：由于链路自适应机制，上行MCS保持稳定，PRB数量准确反映IP包长度。利用该侧信道设计Crosshair攻击，仅通过观测上行频谱即可重建双向流量，无需解码任何信道，在应用识别精度上超越现有SOTA方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 5G对控制信道进行了加密，打破了传统利用蜂窝控制信道元数据推断应用活动（Air fingerprinting）的攻击链。作者试图寻找一种能够绕过5G加密的物理层侧信道，实现应用活动推断。

Method: 利用链路自适应机制下MCS稳定、PRB数量反映IP包长度的物理层侧信道，设计Crosshair三步攻击：(1) 盲提取阶段：从IQ样本中通过能量检测恢复上行物理信道占用，从上行频谱重建双向流量；(2) 数据增强：合成跨信道条件的频谱分布，消除先验环境知识需求；(3) 跨模态对齐：将频谱域与IP域嵌入共享空间，支持仅凭IP流量注册新应用。

Result: 在5G NR测试平台上的实验表明，Crosshair在应用识别精度上超越现有SOTA物理层指纹识别方法，在跨MCS场景下保持高准确率。

Conclusion: 5G的加密措施无法完全防御侧信道攻击，物理层资源分配模式（PRB数量）仍可作为有效的侧信道泄露源，威胁用户隐私。

Abstract: Air fingerprinting infers application activity by sniffing metadata from cellular control channels. 5G encrypts these channels, breaking the attack chain that prior attacks depend on. This paper reveals a physical-layer side channel that bypasses encryption: under the link adaptation mandated by the cellular communication standard, the uplink Modulation and Coding Scheme (MCS) remains stable, so the number of Physical Resource Blocks (PRBs) occupied by a transmission accurately reflects the IP packet length. Combined with the uplink control channel that carries downlink information, an attacker can reconstruct a bidirectional traffic profile. This bidirectional information recovery can be achieved simply by observing the uplink spectrum, without decoding any channel. Building on this side channel, we design Crosshair, a passive three-step attack. First, a blind extraction stage recovers the uplink physical channel occupancy from raw IQ samples via energy detection, reconstructing bidirectional traffic from uplink spectrum. Second, we design a data augmentation method that synthesizes spectral profiles across diverse channel conditions, eliminating the need for prior knowledge of the communication environment. Third, cross-modal alignment embeds the spectral and IP domains into a shared space, enabling new applications to be enrolled from a collected IP trace alone. Extensive experiments on a 5G NR testbed demonstrate the robustness and precision of Crosshair: it outperforms the State-of-the-Art (SOTA) physical layer fingerprinting method in application recognition accuracy, and maintains high accuracy in cross-MCS scenarios.

</details>


### [153] [LLM: LSTM Look-Ahead Moving Target Defense Based on Historical Malicious Scan](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15229)
*Yu Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 针对IP shuffling移动目标防御面临的扩展性差、配置开销高、需大量未用地址块三大挑战，首次提出基于LSTM网络预测攻击者扫描目标的LLM方法，结合集成学习与动态变异机制，在安全性和开销上优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于IP shuffling的移动目标防御存在三个关键挑战：1) 在不同网络拓扑上扩展性有限；2) 即使没有主动攻击也存在固有的重新配置开销；3) 需要大规模未使用的地址块。因此需要一种更高效、适应性更强的防御方案。

Method: 首次使用长短期记忆(LSTM)网络预测攻击者可能扫描的未来目标地址，采用集成学习提高对不同扫描行为的鲁棒性，并引入动态变异机制增强适应性。

Result: 与基线变异策略相比，LLM在安全性和开销两方面均表现更优。

Conclusion: LLM方法有效解决了现有IP shuffling防御的三大挑战，通过预测性地址变异在保障安全性的同时降低了系统开销，为移动目标防御提供了一种新的智能优化方向。

Abstract: Network scanning is a critical preliminary step for most adversaries to gain essential information before launching cyber attacks. Moving Target Defense (MTD) based on IP shuffling has emerged as a proactive defense strategy to counteract these reconnaissance efforts. Unlike static, reactive defense techniques, IP shuffling introduces randomness by dynamically reassigning network addresses, making it more challenging for attackers to identify and track targets. However, current IP shuffling methods face three key challenges: 1) limited scalability across different network topologies, 2) inherent reconfiguration overhead even in the absence of an active attack, and 3) the need for large-scale unused address blocks. To address these issues, we propose LSTM Look-ahead Moving Target Defense (LLM). Our approach is the first attempt using a Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM) network to predict future target addresses that attackers will likely scan. Ensemble learning is used to improve robustness to different scanning behaviors. We introduce a dynamic mutation mechanism to enhance adaptability. Compared to the baseline mutation strategy, LLM performs better in both security and overhead.

</details>


### [154] [Benign in Isolation, Harmful in Composition: Security Risks in Agent Skill Ecosystems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15242)
*Yi Xie,Jiawei Du,Yu Cheng,Jiuan Zhou,Zhaoxia Yin*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了技能组合风险（SCR）的概念，即多个看似无害的技能在共享执行上下文中组合使用时可能产生安全危害。为此构建了SCR-Bench基准测试，包含三个子基准（SCR-CapFlow、SCR-TrustLift、SCR-AuthBlur），实验结果证明组合路径下的攻击成功率远高于孤立评估，表明智能体技能安全应在激活路径层面而非孤立工件层面进行评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有技能审查通常孤立评估每个技能，而真实智能体任务往往在共享执行上下文中调用多个技能。这种孤立评估方式无法捕捉技能组合使用时可能产生的安全风险，如数据泄露、未授权操作和工具滥用等。

Method: 构建SCR-Bench基准测试，包含三个子基准：(1) SCR-CapFlow：评估能力流组合风险；(2) SCR-TrustLift：评估信任传递组合风险；(3) SCR-AuthBlur：评估授权混淆组合风险。采用沙盒化技能环境，记录下游状态变化和路径级结果，而非仅依赖文本意图或表面行为。

Result: (1) SCR-CapFlow中组合攻击成功率达33.6%，孤立基线接近零；(2) SCR-TrustLift中四个后端的攻击成功率超过96.5%；(3) SCR-AuthBlur中L1上下文设置的冒险批准率相比L0孤立基线增加71.8%。

Conclusion: 智能体技能安全应在激活路径层面而非孤立工件层面进行评估。SCR和SCR-Bench为LLM智能体技能生态中的路径感知风险评估和防御提供了基础。

Abstract: Skills are becoming the capability layer through which LLM agents turn plans into actions, but their use introduces security risks such as data leakage, unauthorized operations, and tool misuse. Existing vetting usually evaluates each skill in isolation, while real agent tasks often invoke multiple skills in a shared execution context. This creates Skill Composition Risk (SCR): a skill that appears benign alone can become harmful when its outputs, trust signals, authorization cues, or side effects influence later invocations along an activated path. We introduce SCR-Bench to evaluate this risk in controlled, sandboxed skill environments. Rather than relying only on textual intent or surface behavior, SCR-Bench records downstream state changes and path-level outcomes across composed skill executions. It contains three sub-benchmarks: SCR-CapFlow for capability-flow composition, SCR-TrustLift for trust-transfer composition, and SCR-AuthBlur for authorization-confusion composition. Across SCR-Bench, composed paths expose risks that are largely absent under isolated evaluation. In SCR-CapFlow, attack success rate reaches 33.6 percent under composition, compared with near-zero isolated baselines. In SCR-TrustLift, attack success rate exceeds 96.5 percent on four of five backends. In SCR-AuthBlur, the risky-approval rate increases by 71.8 percent relative to the L0 isolated baseline under the L1 context setting. These results show that agent skill security should be assessed at the level of activated paths rather than isolated artifacts. SCR and SCR-Bench provide a foundation for path-aware risk evaluation and defense in LLM agent skill ecosystems. Benchmark: https://github.com/saint-viperx/SCR_Bench.

</details>


### [155] [Defending against Adaptive Prompt Injection Attacks via Reasoning-enabled Task Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15441)
*Lipeng He,Yihan Wang,Jiawen Zhang,N. Asokan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: RETA是一种基于训练的防御方法，通过将防御决策 grounded 在用户任务上而非攻击者控制的数据上，结合链式推理验证、红队攻击模拟和字典学习多样性奖励，以及多目标强化学习优化，在六种黑盒自适应攻击下将平均攻击成功率降至2.92%-3.75%，同时保持良好效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对LLM代理的间接提示注入防御在静态基准测试中表现优异，但面对攻击者可针对防御进行优化的自适应攻击时，防御效果急剧下降。作者将这一失败归因于两个缺陷：一是防御方法仅识别特定攻击模式而非评估指令与用户任务的相关性；二是基于训练的防御仅使用少量人工模板生成对抗样本，导致泛化能力不足。

Method: 提出RETA方法，包含三个关键组件：(1) 在每次工具输出步骤，防御者通过链式推理验证其行为与用户任务的一致性；(2) 利用红队模拟攻击者合成对抗训练数据，并引入字典学习多样性奖励以覆盖更广泛的注入策略；(3) 通过多目标强化学习优化防御模型，实现更好的安全-效用权衡。

Result: 在六种黑盒自适应攻击下，RETA将每种攻击的成功率均控制在10%以下，在两个目标模型上的平均攻击成功率分别为2.92%和3.75%，同时在遭受攻击和干净输入场景下均保持了大部分效用。

Conclusion: RETA通过将防御决策与用户任务对齐、利用多样化对抗训练和多目标优化，有效解决了现有防御在自适应攻击下的脆弱性问题，实现了更强的安全-效用平衡。

Abstract: Indirect prompt injection attacks hijack LLM-based agents by embedding malicious instructions in third-party data that the agent retrieves during task execution. Existing defenses report near-zero attack success rate on static benchmarks, yet recent adaptive evaluations show that these results collapse once the attacker is allowed to optimize against the deployed defense. In this work, we trace this collapse to two failure modes. First, existing defense methods are confined to recognizing specific attack patterns, rather than assessing whether the intent of every embedded instruction is relevant to the user task. Second, training-based defenses, which otherwise offer the strongest safety-utility trade-off, assemble their adversarial examples from a handful of hand-crafted templates, and the resulting defender fails to generalize outside that narrow strategy distribution. To address these gaps, we propose RETA, a training-based method that grounds defense decisions on the user tasks rather than attacker-controlled data. At each tool-output step, the defender undertakes chain-of-thought reasoning verifying that its actions are consistent with the user task. Leveraging red-teaming, a simulated attacker synthesizes adversarial training data and receives a dictionary-learning diversity reward, achieving broad coverage of injection-reformulation strategies. Together, these allow the defender to be optimized via multi-objective reinforcement learning and achieve better safety-utility trade-off. Across six black-box adaptive attacks, RETA keeps every per-attack ASR below 10%, with average ASR of 2.92% and 3.75% on the two target models, while preserving most utility under attack and on clean inputs.

</details>


### [156] [The Audit Gap in Blockchain Security: A Four-Year Empirical Study of Public Audit Findings and Real-World Exploit Incidents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15465)
*Stefan Beyer*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文对2022年1月1日至2026年3月27日期间Web3安全格局进行了实证分析，整合了23,818份公开审计报告和218起真实世界攻击事件（总损失约77.6亿美元），发现三个核心结论：审计发现严重性分布稳定（Critical+High占比始终在15-17%）、审计发现与攻击损失分类不匹配（私钥泄露/钓鱼/社会工程攻击占损失49.6%但审计发现占比极小）、损失高度集中（前8大事件占总损失50.6%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究缺少对Web3安全审计与真实世界攻击事件的系统性对比分析，无法回答审计发现是否反映实际安全风险这一关键问题。

Method: 收集22家独立安全公司的23,818份公开审计结果和rekt.news记录的218起攻击事件（总损失约77.6亿美元），将审计输出与攻击输出作为不同总体进行平行分析，而非直接比较样本。

Result: 1）审计发现按严重性、类别和技术栈的分布在观察期内基本稳定，Critical+High占比保持在15-17%区间；2）攻击损失类别分布与审计发现类别分布不对应，私钥泄露/钓鱼/社会工程攻击占累计损失49.6%但在审计发现中占比极小；3）实际损失高度集中，前8大事件占50.6%，前20大事件占71.4%，分布形态不符合高斯假设。

Conclusion: 审计发现与真实攻击事件描述的是不同总体，应并行分析而非直接比较；Web3安全审计存在系统性盲区，未能有效覆盖造成主要损失的攻击向量。

Abstract: This paper presents an empirical analysis of the Web3 security landscape over the four-year and three-month period from 1 January 2022 to 27 March 2026. The dataset combines 23,818 public audit findings produced by 22 independent security firms with 218 real-world exploit incidents documented by rekt.news, representing aggregate losses of approximately US$7.76 billion. We report three central findings. First, the distribution of audit findings (by severity, category, and technology stack) is substantially stable across the observation window, with the Critical-plus-High share remaining within a 15-17% band in every complete year. Second, the categorical distribution of realised exploit losses does not correspond to the categorical distribution of audit findings: private-key compromise, phishing, and social-engineering vectors account for approximately 49.6% of cumulative losses yet represent a negligible share of published audit findings. Third, realised losses exhibit extreme concentration: the eight largest incidents account for 50.6% of cumulative dollar losses and the twenty largest for 71.4%, a distributional shape inconsistent with Gaussian assumptions. Throughout, we adopt the analytical convention that audit outputs and exploit outputs describe different populations and present the two datasets in parallel rather than as directly comparable samples.

</details>


### [157] [Multi-tier Differential Private Query Release](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15543)
*Shaowei Wang,Jinn Li,Yun Peng,Puning Zhao,Wenqi Ren,Changyu Dong,Jin Li,Jian Weng*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多层级差分隐私查询发布框架，能够在不同隐私预算下同时满足累积隐私损失不超过最大预算、并达到与单层机制相当的最优效用，适用于噪声添加机制和本地差分隐私模型等多种场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多层级差分隐私查询方法要么累积隐私损失过大，要么效用次优。本文旨在设计一种框架，在多个分析师以不同隐私预算查询同一统计问题时，同时控制隐私损失并实现最优效用。

Method: 针对噪声添加机制（如基于特征函数的双边几何机制），提出基于噪声分布特征函数的通用解法；针对本地差分隐私模型（如子集机制），设计机制专属的预算变换原语和模板化策略，实现不同隐私体制下的最优效用。

Result: 实验结果表明，所提框架在多层级差分隐私查询发布中有效控制了累积隐私损失，并实现了接近单层机制的最优效用。

Conclusion: 本文提出的多层级DP查询发布框架能够同时保证隐私损失上限为最大隐私预算、并达到最优效用，适用于多种DP机制，具有广泛的应用价值。

Abstract: Answering statistical queries over sensitive data under differential privacy (DP) is a common task in many settings, including databases, mobile computing, and data markets. In these scenarios, multiple analysts may issue the same query, while receiving answers generated under different privacy budgets due to differences in trust levels or willingness to pay. Existing approaches for such multi-tier DP queries either incur excessive cumulative privacy loss or suffer from suboptimal utility. In this paper, we propose a framework for multi-tier DP query release that simultaneously bound the cumulative privacy loss by the maximum privacy budget among all queries and achieve optimal utility comparable to that of single-tier mechanisms. Our framework applies to different classes of DP mechanisms. For noise-adding mechanisms (e.g., count queries with the two-sided Geometric mechanism in the curator model), we develop a general solution based on the characteristic functions of noise distributions. For other mechanisms (e.g., count queries under the local DP model with the Subset mechanism), we design mechanism-specific primitives for budget transformation and introduce a template-based strategy that attains optimal utility across different privacy regimes. Experimental results demonstrate the effectiveness of our framework.

</details>


### [158] [CmdNeedle: Measuring the Incompleteness of Command Denylists for AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15549)
*Chuyang Chen,Zhiqiang Lin*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次系统性地刻画了终端AI智能体中命令黑名单的脆弱性问题，提出了LLM驱动管道CmdNeedle来检测此类脆弱性，并对1,709个真实世界命令黑名单进行评估，发现69.0-98.6%的黑名单存在脆弱性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 终端AI智能体依赖shell命令执行与主机系统交互，采用三列表命令门控机制（其中黑名单是核心组件）来缓解安全风险。然而，即使像Claude Code这样由开发者精心维护的内置黑名单也会遗漏绕过命令，导致黑名单脆弱无效，无法阻止实际操作中应被禁止的操作。

Method: 形式化定义了命令黑名单脆弱性问题，提出LLM驱动管道CmdNeedle：利用LLM提出可能的绕过方案，并通过验证器在沙箱中执行这些绕过方案以获取反馈，迭代修复黑名单。

Result: 从GitHub收集了1,709个真实世界命令黑名单（包含13,332条黑名单规则），评估发现69.0-98.6%的黑名单存在脆弱性，且该脆弱性在不同项目和智能体间一致存在，同时验证了若干可能的根本原因。

Conclusion: 提出的管道和发现将有助于未来关于AI智能体命令黑名单的研究和实践。

Abstract: The adoption of AI agents is increasing rapidly. Terminal AI agents, i.e., AI agents that run in terminal environments, are a widely used type of AI agents. Terminal AI agents rely heavily on shell command execution to interact with the host systems. They adopt a three-list command-gating mechanism to mitigate security risks introduced by command execution, with denylists serving as the load-bearing component. However, modern operating systems often ship a large, ever-expanding set of shell commands with complex functionalities. Our observation is that even a built-in denylist of Claude Code, well-maintained by its developers, can overlook bypass commands that invalidate its effectiveness. Such negligence leads to fragile command denylists that cannot even block operations that practitioners expect them to block.
  This paper presents the first systematic characterization of command denylist fragility in terminal AI agents. The paper formalizes the command denylist fragility problem and proposes an LLM-driven pipeline, CmdNeedle, to detect such fragility. It prompts the LLM to propose possible bypasses and iteratively repairs them using feedback from a validator that executes them in a sandbox. In the evaluation, we applied CmdNeedle to 1,709 real-world command denylists (containing 13,332 denylist rules) collected from GitHub. The evaluation shows several key findings, including that 69.0--98.6% of the denylists are fragile, that this fragility occurs consistently across projects and agents, and the validity of several possible root causes for this fragility. Our pipeline and findings will hopefully facilitate future research and practice regarding the command denylists used by AI agents.

</details>


### [159] [FragFuse: Bypassing Access Control of Large Language Model Agents via Memory-Based Query Fragmentation and Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15609)
*Zixin Rao,Wentian Zhu,Chan Aristella Lu,Zhaorun Chen,Wei Niu,Le Guan,Bo Li,Zhen Xiang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出FragFuse攻击，利用LLM智能体的长期记忆机制，通过将违禁内容分片存储于记忆后重组，绕过访问控制。在多个代理设置和任务域中，平均绕过成功率达86.3%，端到端有害任务成功率达41.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体依赖长期记忆支持复杂任务，同时访问控制机制被用于阻止违规请求。作者发现记忆操作引入新攻击面：违禁内容可在交互中分片、以无害形式存储于长期记忆，后续通过检索重组，绕过显式查询中的访问控制。

Method: FragFuse包含三阶段：(1) 通过黑盒自适应查询与片段掩码识别拒绝响应片段；(2) 使用标记载体查询将片段注入记忆；(3) 通过后续攻击查询检索并融合存储片段。进一步提出基于替代模型的优化方案，自动调整融合指令和标记设计。

Result: 在四种代理设置和任务域中，覆盖三种SOTA访问控制机制，平均绕过成功率86.3%，端到端有害任务成功率41.1%，相比无访问控制配置仅下降4.4%。现有提示注入检测器和困惑度检测器均无法有效防御。

Conclusion: 长期记忆机制引入的时间通道使未授权用户可绕过智能体访问控制，现有防御手段对此攻击无效，需设计针对记忆操作的新防御策略。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents increasingly rely on long-term memory to support complex task execution, user personalization, and domain adaptation. Meanwhile, emerging access-control mechanisms for LLM agents are being explored to block policy-violating requests and prevent misuse. We reveal a novel attack surface arising from agent memory operations: prohibited content that would trigger access control can be fragmented across interactions, stored in long-term memory in benign-appearing form, and later reconstructed through memory retrieval without appearing explicitly in the final user query. We propose FragFuse, the first attack that enables unprivileged users to bypass agent access control by exploiting this temporal channel introduced by long-term memory. FragFuse operates in three stages: (1) identifying rejection-responsive fragments via black-box adaptive querying with fragment masking; (2) injecting these fragments into memory using marker carrier queries; and (3) retrieving and fusing the stored fragments through a follow-up attack query. Although FragFuse can be instantiated manually for individual agents, we further develop a surrogate-based optimization scheme that tunes fusion instructions and marker designs, enabling automated attack generation without violating the attacker's threat-model assumptions. We evaluate FragFuse across four representative agent settings and task domains, covering three state-of-the-art agent access-control mechanisms. FragFuse achieves an average bypass success rate of 86.3% and an average end-to-end harmful task success rate of 41.1% across all settings, with only 4.4% average task-success degradation compared with configurations without access control. We also show that alternative defenses, including state-of-the-art prompt-injection detectors and perplexity detectors, do not effectively address this attack.

</details>


### [160] [AnonShield: Scalable On-Premise Pseudonymization for CSIRT Vulnerability Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15650)
*Cristhian Kapelinski,Douglas Lautert,Beatriz Machado,Diego Kreutz,Isadora Garcia Ferrão*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: AnonShield是一个基于GPU加速的高吞吐量伪匿名化系统，可在CSIRT环境中实现高效、合规的漏洞数据共享，处理速度提升最高738倍，F1分数达94.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有伪匿名化系统在处理大规模漏洞数据时性能不足，难以满足CSIRT（计算机安全事件响应团队）环境对高吞吐量和低延迟的需求，且需要在数据可用性与隐私保护之间取得平衡。

Method: 结合GPU加速的命名实体识别（NER）、流式处理、缓存机制和模式感知配置，构建了一个高性能的本地化伪匿名化系统。

Result: 在最大550MB（70,951条记录）的数据集上，处理时间从92小时以上降至10分钟以内（最高738倍加速），F1分数达到94.2%，召回率达到96.7%。

Conclusion: 可扩展的漏洞数据伪匿名化在不牺牲分析效用的前提下是可行的，能够支持CSIRT环境中的合规数据共享。

Abstract: We present AnonShield, a high-throughput, on-premise pseudonymization system that combines GPU-accelerated NER, streaming processing, caching, and schema-aware configuration. Evaluated on datasets up to 550 MB (70,951 records), AnonShield reduces processing time from over 92 hours to under 10 minutes (up to 738x speedup) while achieving up to 94.2% F1-score and 96.7% recall. Our results show that scalable pseudonymization of vulnerability data is feasible without sacrificing analytical utility, enabling compliant data sharing in operational CSIRT environments.

</details>


### [161] [Odds Law: The Decomposition Algebra On How Intelligence Organizes Itself to Solve Difficult Problems Reliably](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15712)
*Hidayet Aksu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种分解代数，通过四种组合子（顺序组合、并行集成、验证门控和递归约简）从不可靠的基本求解器构建可靠复合求解器，并证明了验证赔率定律、可靠性放大定理和阈值二分法，揭示了可靠性通过独立信息和结构化组合实现放大的基本规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 给定不可靠的基本问题求解器，什么样的组织结构能使它们可靠地解决难题？以及这种可靠性的极限在哪里？这是对可靠性与结构之间关系的根本性结构性问题探索。

Method: 建立分解代数框架：将基本求解器视为随机范畴中的态射，四种组合子生成复合求解器空间；引入可靠性估值（到有序幺半群[0,1]的同态）和成本估值（到交换半环的同态），推导可靠性在结构中流动的组合定律；通过单调改进算子的最小不动点刻画自组织过程。

Result: (i) 验证赔率定律：验证门将正确赔率乘以验证器的似然比Λ，k个条件独立门产生几何级放大；(ii) 可靠性放大定理：当Λ>1时，达到目标可靠性1-δ只需O(log 1/δ)的验证深度；(iii) 阈值二分法：高于临界参数时可靠性可逼近1且成本对数增长，低于或等于时无法放大；(iv) 信息天花板和投票地板：每个门的放大受散度量限制，共享错误导致严格正的投票地板，多样性对无限放大是必要的。

Conclusion: 可靠性既非免费也非魔法：它需要用独立的信息购买，通过组合来安排，并受限于验证器的能力。自组织达到边际对数赔率增益与单位成本相等的均衡点。信息的独立性和多样性是实现可靠性放大的必要条件。

Abstract: We ask a structural question: given unreliable elementary problem-solvers, what organizations of them solve hard problems reliably, and what are the limits? We develop a $decomposition~algebra$: elementary solvers are morphisms in a stochastic category, and four combinators (sequential composition, parallel ensembling, verification gating, and recursive reduction) generate the space of compound solvers. We equip this algebra with two homomorphisms, a $reliability$ valuation into the ordered monoid $([0,1],\le)$ and a $cost$ valuation into a commutative semiring, and we derive the composition laws that govern how reliability flows through structure. Our central results are (i) a $verification~odds~law$ (the result that names this report), showing that a verification gate multiplies the odds of correctness by the verifier's likelihood ratio $Λ$, so that $k$ conditionally independent gates yield geometric amplification; (ii) a $reliability~amplification~theorem$, giving target reliability $1-δ$ at $O(\log 1/δ)$ verification depth whenever $Λ>1$; and (iii) a $threshold~dichotomy$: above the critical parameters reliability can be driven arbitrarily close to one at logarithmic cost, while at or below them no amplification is possible. We then show that $self-organization$ is the least fixed point of a monotone improvement operator on the complete lattice of strategies, and that this fixed point equalizes marginal log-odds gain per unit cost. Finally, we prove matching limits: an information ceiling bounds per-gate amplification by a divergence quantity; shared error causes create a strictly positive voting floor, so diversity is $necessary$ for unbounded amplification. Reliability, in short, is neither free nor magical: it is bought with independent information, arranged by composition, and bounded by the verifier.

</details>


### [162] [Snyk VulnBench JS 1.0: Can LLMs Find the Same Bugs Twice?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15762)
*Liran Tal,Johannes Kloos,Arsenii Rudich,Stephen Thoemmes,Manoj Nair*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文通过对同一JavaScript代码、提示和基准测试框架进行300次重复漏洞扫描，发现LLM安全审查的可重复性不均衡：参考匹配的发现结果稳定，但额外模型报告的结果在不同运行间变化很大。Claude匹配Snyk Code参考发现时行为稳定，而80个唯一未匹配发现中有80个仅出现在五次重复中的一次。Snyk Code SAST是确定性的，能更系统地枚举重复数据流汇点。研究支持将智能体LLM审查与确定性SAST结合使用，而非相互替代。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究智能体LLM在安全代码审查中的可重复性，以及其与确定性SAST工具的互补性，为实际安全工具选型提供依据。

Method: 在相同JavaScript代码、提示和基准测试框架下，进行300次重复漏洞扫描，分析LLM（Claude）发现结果的稳定性和变异模式，并与确定性SAST工具Snyk Code进行对比。

Result: LLM安全发现的可重复性不均衡：参考匹配的发现稳定（134/158出现在全部五次重复中），但额外模型报告结果高度变异（80/161唯一未匹配发现仅出现在一次重复中）。Snyk Code SAST确定性地枚举数据流汇点。LLM能发现高信号漏洞模式，甚至发现了Snyk Code的产品空白。

Conclusion: 智能体LLM审查与确定性SAST具有互补性，应结合使用而非相互替代，LLM擅长发现高信号漏洞但稳定性差，SAST系统但缺乏灵活性。

Abstract: We ran 300 repeated vulnerability-finding scans to measure how repeatable agentic large language model (LLM) security review is on the same JavaScript code, prompt, and benchmark harness. The headline result is that LLM security findings were unevenly repeatable: reference-matched findings were stable, but extra model reports varied heavily from run to run. Across 250 model runs, 80 of 161 unique unmatched findings appeared in only one of five identical repetitions, while only 22 appeared in all five. By contrast, when Claude matched a Snyk Code reference finding, the behavior was much more stable: 134 of 158 unique reference-matched findings appeared in all five repetitions. The benchmark also shows complementarity. Models consistently found familiar, high-signal exploit shapes, and in one case surfaced a likely Snyk Code product gap. Snyk Code static application security testing (SAST) was deterministic and better at systematically enumerating repeated data-flow sinks. The results support combining agentic LLM review with deterministic SAST rather than treating either technique as a replacement for the other.

</details>


### [163] [GAS-Leak-LLM: Genetic Algorithm-Based Suffix Optimization for Black-Box LLM Jailbreaking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15788)
*Aman Anifer,Vignesh Kumar Kembu,Vishnu M,Antonino Nocera,Vinod P.,Amal Murali PK,Akshay S Rajan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出GAS-Leak-LLM，一种基于遗传算法的黑盒越狱攻击方法，通过迭代进化对抗性后缀来绕过LLM的安全约束，实验证明了该方法有效且暴露了现有安全机制的缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLM采用了高级对齐策略和多层内容审核机制，但仍易受对抗性操控（越狱攻击和提示注入）。现有攻击方法需要白盒访问或计算成本过高，亟需一种更贴近现实威胁场景（严格黑盒设置）的高效攻击方法，以揭示安全机制的不足。

Method: 提出GAS-Leak-LLM框架，在完全黑盒设定下（无需模型参数或内部信息），将对抗性后缀的搜索建模为离散提示空间的演化优化问题。通过迭代应用选择（selection）、变异（mutation）和交叉（crossover）三类遗传算法启发式操作，系统性地进化对抗性后缀以最大化使模型生成违规内容的适应度（fitness）。

Result: 实证研究证实了该方法在实际部署系统中的有效性和可行性，成功绕过了现有安全约束机制，揭示了当前LLM安全防护措施存在严重缺陷。

Conclusion: GAS-Leak-LLM作为一种黑盒越狱攻击，展示了遗传算法在对抗性提示攻击中的实用价值，凸显了当前LLM安全对齐机制在面对进化式对抗搜索时的脆弱性，为改进LLM安全防御提供了重要参考。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) constitute pivotal components within the AI-dominated information technology ecosystem. To mitigate risks associated with harmful or policy-violating outputs, commercial systems employ advanced alignment strategies and multi-layered content moderation mechanisms. Despite these safeguards, recent research has demonstrated that LLMs remain vulnerable to adversarial manipulation, particularly through jailbreaking and prompt injection techniques. In this work, we propose GAS-Leak-LLM a novel jailbreaking attack based on a genetic algorithm that systematically evolves adversarial suffix to bypass safety constraints. Operating in a strict black-box setting, our method requires no access to model parameters or internals, thereby reflecting realistic threat scenarios in deployed systems. Through the iterative application of selection, mutation, and crossover heuristics, the framework systematically explores the discrete prompt space to identify high-fitness adversarial suffixes. Empirical findings reveal critical shortcomings in existing safety enforcement mechanisms and confirm the effectiveness and practical viability of the proposed attack.

</details>


### [164] [AttackonCTF: Defending Hardware Security Competition Benchmarks in the Age of LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15809)
*Mohamadreza Rostami,Nikhilesh Singh,Stephen Muttathil,Lichao Wu,Chen Chen,Huimin Li,Jeyavijayan Rajendran,Ahmad-Reza Sadeghi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 研究发现LLMs在硬件安全竞赛中并非通过真正的安全推理，而是利用diff风格的语法比较达到83%的检测率，破坏了基准测试的有效性。为此提出了首个面向LLM的语义保留混淆框架，通过人可读的变换和受控的diff噪声，在HackTheSilicon上将LLM检测准确率降低50%（10%混淆度）至78.6%（完全混淆），恢复了基准测试的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硬件安全竞赛（如HackTheSilicon）作为评估漏洞检测方法和训练人类/AI的基准平台，其有效性受到威胁——研究发现LLMs并非进行真正的安全推理，而是利用diff风格的语法比较来检测漏洞，破坏了公平评估。

Method: 提出首个面向LLM的语义保留混淆框架，与IP保护方法不同，它应用人可读的变换和受控的diff噪声，同时保持功能完整性。

Result: 在HackTheSilicon上，该框架使LLM检测准确率降低50%（仅10%混淆度），完全混淆下降低78.6%。

Conclusion: 该混淆框架有效恢复了基准测试的可靠性，防止LLMs利用语法捷径而非真正的安全推理进行漏洞检测。

Abstract: Hardware security competitions such as HackTheSilicon serve as benchmarking platforms for evaluating vulnerability detection methods and for training humans and AI. However, our study reveals that LLMs threaten their validity. Instead of genuine security reasoning, detectors exploit a diff-style syntactic comparison, achieving an 83% detection rate, undermining fair evaluation. To mitigate this, we propose the first LLM-oriented, semantics-preserving obfuscation framework for these benchmarks. Unlike IP-protection approaches, it applies human-readable transformations and controlled diff-noise while preserving functionality. On HackTheSilicon, the framework reduces LLM-based detection accuracy by 50% with only 10% obfuscation and by 78.6% under complete obfuscation, restoring benchmark reliability.

</details>


### [165] [Let Them Steal: Trapping Large Language Model Extraction Attacks with Knowledge Honeypot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15810)
*Yuyang Dai,Yushun Dong*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出Knowledge Trap防御方法，通过蜜罐知识图谱和面包屑引导，将模型提取攻击的查询预算消耗在低迁移性知识上，在医疗和金融领域使替代模型一致性平均降低6.2%而不影响合法用户准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 商业API部署的大语言模型容易遭受模型提取攻击，现有防御要么反应太迟，要么损害合法用户的效用。

Method: 构建蜜罐知识图谱（HKG）并采用面包屑引导探索，将攻击者的有限查询预算引导至下游效用极低的知识上，而非直接拦截查询或扰动输出。

Result: 在医疗和金融领域，替代模型一致性平均降低6.2%，合法用户准确率不受影响，优于现有存在可测量用户影响的防御方法。

Conclusion: 防御知识空间遍历是缓解LLM提取攻击的实用方向。

Abstract: Large language models deployed as commercial APIs are vulnerable to model extraction attacks, while existing defenses either act too late or degrade utility for legitimate users. We propose \textbf{Knowledge Trap}, a defense that redirects extraction attacks toward low-transferability knowledge through a \emph{Honeypot Knowledge Graph} (HKG) and breadcrumb-guided exploration. Instead of blocking queries or perturbing outputs, Knowledge Trap consumes the attacker's limited query budget on knowledge with negligible downstream utility while preserving benign-user performance. Experiments in medical and financial domains show that Knowledge Trap reduces surrogate Agreement by 6.2\% on average without degrading legitimate-user accuracy, outperforming existing defenses that impose measurable user impact. These results suggest that defending knowledge-space traversal is a practical direction for mitigating LLM extraction attacks.

</details>


### [166] [FuseChain: Runtime Evidence Reconstruction for Software Supply-Chain Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15811)
*Zhuoran Tan,Yutian Tang,Jeremy Singer,Christos Anagnostopoulos,Ke Xiao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: FUSECHAIN是一个运行时检测框架，通过将多源软件供应链遥测数据对齐到统一事件时间轴上的时序异构图，实现跨源攻击痕迹的关联与攻击阶段重建，实验表明解耦异常检测与阶段预测能有效提升部署性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件供应链攻击日益呈现多阶段、跨来源、时间分布的特点，单一来源遥测中的攻击证据碎片化且低频，现有运行时检测系统独立分析各数据源，难以识别低频攻击证据或重建时序上下文。

Method: 将多源遥测（包/运行时轨迹、进程事件、网络遥测、DNS/HTTP元数据、安全告警）对齐到统一事件时间轴，构建时序异质属性图；从良性前缀遥测中学习以异常为中心的时序表示；通过冻结异常骨干网络上的轻量化解码器进行可部署的攻击阶段重建。

Result: 在7个SSC攻击场景下，冻结骨干解码器将可部署阶段重建的Stage Recall@500从0.369提升至0.881；自适应检索进一步将可观察阶段召回率从0.524提升至0.655。

Conclusion: 在稀疏且不平衡的运行时供应链遥测数据中，联合优化异常检测与阶段预测效果不佳；解耦运行时SSC异常检测与下游攻击阶段解释具有实际的部署价值。

Abstract: Software supply-chain (SSC) attacks are increasingly multi-stage, cross-source, and temporally distributed. A single attack campaign may leave weak and fragmented traces across multi-source telemetry that captures different granularities and perspectives of runtime behavior. Existing runtime detection systems often analyze these sources independently, making it difficult to identify low-frequency attack evidence or reconstruct the temporal context in which it appears. We present FUSECHAIN, a runtime detection framework that represents multi-source software supply-chain telemetry as a temporal heterogeneous provenance graph over a unified event-time axis. By aligning package/runtime traces, process events, network telemetry, DNS/HTTP metadata, and security alerts on a unified temporal graph, FuseChain captures cross-source dependencies and sparse attack evidence that may be ambiguous within any individual source. It learns anomaly-centric temporal representations from benign-prefix telemetry and performs deployable attack-stage reconstruction through a lightweight decoder on top of a frozen anomaly backbone. Our experiments show that jointly optimizing anomaly detection and stage prediction is ineffective under sparse and imbalanced runtime supply-chain telemetry. Across seven SSC attack scenarios, FuseChain improves deployable stage reconstruction from 0.369 to 0.881 Stage Recall@500 with a frozen-backbone decoder, while adaptive retrieval further increases observable-stage recall from 0.524 to 0.655 without modifying the detector. These results highlight the deployable value of decoupling runtime SSC anomaly detection from downstream attack-stage interpretation.

</details>


### [167] [SkillVetBench: LLM-as-Judge for Multi-Dimensional Security Risk Evaluation in Open-Source LLM Agent Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15899)
*Ismail Hossain,Sai Puppala,Md Jahangir Alam,Tanzim Ahad,Sajedul Talukder*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: SKILLVETBENCH是一个基于Hugging Face的公共排行榜，使用LLM-as-Judge方法对开源LLM代理生态中的技能进行安全审查，提出五维SARS风险评分指标，在78个恶意技能上实现零假阴性、22个良性技能上零假阳性，显著优于传统静态代码扫描工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开源LLM代理生态快速扩张，但社区贡献的技能（模块化工具定义）缺乏安全审查。现有扫描器仅在代码层运行，对指令层攻击（如提示注入、记忆投毒）和多代理风险存在结构性盲区，亟需语义级、多维度的审查系统。

Method: 提出SKILLVETBENCH框架，核心包括：(1) SARS (Skill Agentic Risk Score)——面向指令遵循系统的五维代理风险加权评分；(2) LLM-as-Judge的语义审查管道；(3) 完整集成CVSS v4.0向量分解；(4) ClawHub双视图——LLM生成审查与官方市场裁决并列展示。

Result: LLM-as-Judge在78个确认恶意技能上零假阴性、22个良性对照上零假阳性；最佳静态基线SKILLSIEVE仍漏检15%；针对指令层类别（提示注入、记忆投毒），传统工具漏检率达89%-100%（如CODEBERT对9个记忆投毒技能全部漏检）；四个LLM评估器间检测率35%-95%，表明生产部署需集成投票。

Conclusion: 基于LLM的语义审查方法能有效检测传统代码级扫描器无法发现的指令层和多代理安全威胁，但不同LLM评估器表现差异大，实际部署应采用集成评分策略。

Abstract: Open-source LLM agent ecosystems are growing rapidly, yet the security of community-contributed skills - modular tool definitions that extend agent capabilities - remains largely unvetted. The gap we fill: existing scanners operate at the code layer and are structurally blind to instruction-layer and multi-agent risk - natural-language directives that hijack an agent, exfiltrate data through encoded side channels, or chain harm across pipelines - so what is needed is a semantic, multi-dimensional vetting system rather than another signature matcher. We present SKILLVETBENCH, a live public leaderboard on Hugging Face that uses an LLM-as-Judge to vet agent skills. What is new: SARS (Skill Agentic Risk Score), a five-dimensional agentic-risk metric with a principled weighted formula for instruction-following systems. What is integrated: full CVSS v4.0 vector decomposition and a ClawHub dual-view that places our LLM-generated review beside the official marketplace verdict. What is demonstrated: drawing on our companion benchmark paper [ 1], the LLM-as-Judge stage achieves zero false negatives across 78 confirmed-malicious skills and zero false positives across 22 benign controls, while the best static baseline (SKILLSIEVE) still misses 15%; for instruction-layer categories such as Prompt Injection and Memory Poisoning, conventional tools miss between 89% and 100% of threats (e.g., CODEBERT detects none of nine memory-poisoning skills). Detection rates vary from 35% to 95% across four LLM evaluators, motivating ensemble scoring in production deployments.

</details>


### [168] [Stickel-type key exchange with hidden subspaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16021)
*Fintan Costello,Paul Watts*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文证明了所有基于公共子空间结构的Stickel型矩阵密钥交换方案（包括Stickel原始方案、Shpilrain多项式扩展、Nager代数扩展等）均可被多项式时间攻击破解，并提出了一种通过共轭隐藏交换子空间的新密钥建立方案，该方案的安全性可归约到标准NP难问题（Edmonds问题）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Stickel型密钥交换方案曾被认为具有抵抗量子攻击的潜力，但其中公开子空间的结构性弱点尚未被系统分析。作者试图揭示这类方案的普遍脆弱性，并设计一种能够抵御此类攻击的新方案。

Method: 对Stickel型方案进行witness-finding密码分析，利用公共子空间的交换结构构造多项式时间攻击算法；新方案则通过共轭操作隐藏交换子空间，并将安全性归约到Edmonds问题（一种标准NP难问题）。

Result: 所有现有Stickel型方案均可在多项式时间内被破解；新提出的方案能够抵抗该特定公共子空间分析，且其witness-finding问题具有从NP难问题的直接归约。

Conclusion: Stickel型密钥交换方案因公开子空间的交换结构存在根本性安全缺陷，不宜使用；通过共轭隐藏子空间可以构建更安全的矩阵密钥交换方案，其安全性基于NP难问题假设。

Abstract: We give a witness-finding cryptanalysis of Stickel-type key exchange schemes, which involve two-sided multiplication of $n \times n$ matrices over $\mathbb{F}_p$, where these matrices are drawn from public subspaces with a particular commuting structure. This analysis covers Stickel's original proposal , Shpilrain's polynomial extension of that scheme, Nager's algebraic extension of that scheme, and more generally all Stickel-type approaches using public subspaces over matrix algebra in finite fields: all such schemes can be broken in polynomial time. We also describe a new key establishment scheme using two-sided matrix multiplication in which the commuting subspaces used to form the key are hidden via conjugation by private terms, blocking this specific public-subspace analysis; the witness-finding problem in this new scheme has a direct reduction from a standard NP-hard problem (Edmonds' problem).

</details>


### [169] [New Ideas on a New Old Type of Cipher:The Mixed-Radix One-Time Pad](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16040)
*Fabio F. G. Buono*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文形式化了一种2012年预印本中的非传统密码核心思想，提出混合进制一次性密码本（MR-OTP），将其推广到非均匀基，证明其满足香农完美保密性，并指出经典二进制OTP和固定基变体均为其特例。文章还探讨了秘密基是否能降低完美保密所需的密钥熵（不能），最后给出基于密钥滚动的实用会话协议并诚实列出开放问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将2012年预印本中未形式化的非常规密码核心思想进行形式化，并以此为基础将一次性密码本从均匀二进制/固定字母表推广到非均匀混合进制场景，统一和扩展OTP家族。

Method: 形式化定义混合进制表示核心，构建MR-OTP加密方案，通过信息论证明其满足香农完美保密性，分析特例（二进制OTP、固定基OTP），从熵的角度论证秘密基无法降低密钥熵需求，设计基于密钥滚动的实用会话协议。

Result: MR-OTP实现了香农完美保密性；经典二进制OTP和任意字母表固定基OTP均为其特例；秘密基不能降低完美保密所需的密钥熵；密钥滚动协议可在保持完美保密性的前提下实现实用会话通信。

Conclusion: 混合进制一次性密码本是OTP概念的自然且干净的一般化，秘密基不提供熵优势，但MR-OTP在理论统一性和某些应用场景（如非均匀消息空间）中具有价值。

Abstract: In a short 2012 preprint, an unconventional cipher was introduced, now in this note we take that representational core, formalize it, and use it as the basis for a clean generalization of the one-time pad to non-uniform bases, which we call the Mixed-Radix One-Time Pad (MR-OTP). And we prove that the MR-OTP achieves Shannon perfect secrecy, show that the classical binary OTP is exactly the all-bases-equal-2 special case, and that fixed-base variants recover OTPs over arbitrary alphabets. We then examine whether secret bases can lower the key entropy required for perfect secrecy (they cannot). We close with a usable session protocol based on key rolling that preserves perfect secrecy, and with an honest account of the open problems.

</details>


### [170] [The Anatomy of Scam Scenarios: Large-Scale Characterization and Conversation-Aware Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16052)
*Shang Ma,Chen Yanai,Avichai Ben,Zichen Liu,Yanfang Ye,Xusheng Xiao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文通过大规模实证研究，构建了诈骗场景的层次化分类体系（6大策略、18个细粒度场景），揭示了心理技术与诈骗场景的系统性关联，并设计了对话感知的诈骗场景检测方法，用于金融客户交互中的实时预警。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究常将心理技术作为孤立特征分析，忽略了它们被系统化部署的重复性诈骗场景，缺乏对诈骗场景与其关联心理技术的联合表征。

Method: 开发数据驱动的pipeline，从102,054份真实诈骗事件报告中推导出诈骗场景的层次化分类体系，并设计对话感知的诈骗场景检测方法。

Result: 发现心理技术使用与诈骗场景显著关联，诈骗者围绕不同运营目标（广泛暴露、高转化、高价值提取）组织场景，并复用IP、域名、邮箱、电话等基础设施进行规模化攻击。

Conclusion: 场景级知识可有效迁移至实际防御，对话感知的检测方法可实现及时预警和干预。

Abstract: Online scams have become a pervasive global threat, causing substantial financial, psychological, and operational harm. Scammers embed psychological techniques (PTs) within reusable operational schemes to scale scam campaigns with minimal adaptation. However, existing studies often analyze PTs as isolated features, overlooking the recurring scam scenarios in which they are systematically deployed. To address this gap, we first conduct a large-scale empirical study to jointly characterize scam scenarios and their associated PTs. Specifically, we develop a data-driven pipeline to derive a hierarchical taxonomy of scam scenarios, consisting of 18 fine-grained scenarios grouped into 6 high-level tactics based on their PT profiles. Furthermore, to transfer this scenario-level knowledge to practical defense, we design a conversation-aware scam scenario detection approach for financial-institution customer interactions, enabling timely warning and intervention. Our study on 102,054 real-world scam incident reports, spanning 2024-02-01 to 2025-10-31, reveals that PT usage is significantly associated with scam scenarios. We further show that scammers organize scenarios around different operational goals, such as broad victim exposure, high victim conversion, and high-value extraction, and reuse infrastructure, including IP addresses, domains, email addresses, and phone numbers, to launch coordinated campaigns at scale. Evaluation on

</details>


### [171] [MASCOT-Android: A Curated Dataset and Automated Collection Pipeline for Android Malware Source Code Specimens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16072)
*Bojing Li,Duo Zhong,Prajna Bhandary,Raguvir S,Charles Maxa,Robert J Joyce,Charles Nicholas*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了MASCOT-Android，一个Android恶意软件源代码数据集及自动化收集框架。核心发现是仅利用仓库级别的README文档即可有效识别恶意软件仓库，基于字符级TF-IDF特征和LinearSVC分类器实现了96.28%的准确率和1.06%的假阳性率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 恶意软件源代码相比二进制和反编译代码更能直接反映攻击者意图，但源代码稀缺且人工审查成本高，导致此类数据集难以构建和维护。

Method: 提出MASCOT-Android自动化收集框架，从GitHub上发现恶意软件源代码。关键方法是从仓库README文档中提取字符级TF-IDF特征，训练LinearSVC分类器区分恶意软件与良性仓库。模型输出置信度分数，支持用户调整决策阈值平衡假阳性率和覆盖率。

Result: 在8,772个恶意软件和25,747个良性README文档上训练的分类器在本地评估中达到96.28%的准确率和1.06%的假阳性率。

Conclusion: 仅利用仓库级别的README文档即可有效实现大规模恶意软件源代码自动化收集，验证了文档级特征作为强信号的有效性。

Abstract: Compared with binaries and decompiled code, malware source code more directly reflects the attackers' original intent. However, the scarcity of source code and the high cost of manual review make such datasets difficult to build and maintain. We propose MASCOT-Android, a curated dataset of Android malware source code and an automated collection framework for scalable malware source code discovery on GitHub. A key finding of our work is that repository-level documentation alone provides a strong signal for malware source code collection. Our model extracts character-level TF-IDF features from 8,772 malware and 25,747 benign README documents and trains a LinearSVC classifier to distinguish malware repositories. This README-only model achieves an accuracy of 96.28\% and an FPR of 1.06\% in local evaluation. In addition, the model outputs confidence scores, allowing users to adjust the decision threshold to balance FPR and coverage, which is practical in real-world malware source code collection.

</details>


### [172] [Your "Pro" LLM Subscription May Actually Be "Free": Exposing Fingerprint Spoofing Risks in LLM Inference Services](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16100)
*Jiahao Zhang,Xiuyu Li,Suhang Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为"指纹欺骗"的新型威胁，即恶意服务提供商通过参数高效微调弱模型来模仿强模型，从而逃避用户端的黑盒指纹识别。作者从理论上证明了用户端资源限制（有限查询预算和弱分类器）使当前指纹识别易受攻击，并提出了GhostPrint攻击框架，通过代理建模、奖励排序微调和知识蒸馏，使弱模型能够持续绕过代表性指纹识别方法，且微调成本低、效用保持良好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型API的普及，用户依赖黑盒指纹识别来验证提供商是否提供广告中的高级模型。然而，现有方法忽视了可能操纵模型权重来欺骗指纹识别的恶意提供商，因此需要揭示这一安全漏洞。

Method: 首先通过形式化证明揭示用户端资源限制（有限查询预算和弱指纹分类器）使指纹识别易受攻击。然后提出GhostPrint攻击框架，结合代理建模、奖励排序微调（reward-ranked fine-tuning）和知识蒸馏（knowledge distillation）三种技术，对弱模型进行参数高效微调以模仿强模型的行为。

Result: 在静态和持续指纹识别两种设置下的广泛评估表明，GhostPrint能使弱模型持续绕过具有代表性的指纹识别方法，同时保持良好的模型效用，且微调成本较低。

Conclusion: 当前大语言模型指纹识别流程存在关键的脆弱性，指纹欺骗是一种现实可行的安全威胁，亟需更鲁棒的指纹识别防御机制。

Abstract: As Large Language Model (LLM) APIs become ubiquitous, users increasingly rely on black-box fingerprinting to verify that providers are serving the advertised premium models. However, these methods may overlook adversarial providers who manipulate model weights to cheat the fingerprint process. We introduce a novel threat termed fingerprint spoofing, where a malicious provider stealthily serves a weaker model that has been parameter-efficiently fine-tuned to mimic a stronger model, thereby evading user-side fingerprinting. We first formally prove that user-side resource constraints (i.e., finite query budgets and weak fingerprinting classifiers) make current fingerprinting vulnerable to fingerprint spoofing. Guided by this theoretical analysis, we propose GhostPrint, a cost-effective attack framework leveraging surrogate modeling, reward-ranked fine-tuning, and knowledge distillation. Extensive evaluations in both static and continual fingerprinting settings demonstrate that GhostPrint allows weak models to consistently bypass representative fingerprint methods while maintaining utility at a low fine-tuning cost, exposing a critical vulnerability in current LLM fingerprinting pipelines.

</details>


### [173] [Invisible Manipulation Channels in AI-Assisted Financial Advisory: Implications for Market Integrity and Regulatory Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16121)
*Liuyang Yao,Zhouyu Li,Junguang He,Ziyang You*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文发现并验证了LLM推理采样层存在一种隐蔽操控通道，可系统性偏置金融AI的信用评估与投资建议输出，且能绕过包括统计水印在内的所有基于输出的审计机制。基于量子随机数生成器（QRNG）与可信执行环境（TEE）的硬件隔离方案可实现100%攻击阻断，而软件防御完全无效。文章最后提出了四项监管修正建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有监管框架未能充分应对AI系统推理管线的完整性问题，特别是LLM推理采样层存在可被利用的隐蔽漏洞，使攻击者能在保持输出级审计合规的前提下系统性偏置金融意见输出。

Method: 通过理论分析证明操控前后输出分布的KL散度可任意小，使任何基于输出的检测方案需要不可实际的大样本量才能可靠检测；并在信用评级与投资顾问场景下进行实证实验，测试六种黑盒检测器和三种主流水印方案及三种异构模型架构。

Result: 定向偏置关键词在隐蔽操控下可放大1.8-1.9倍，同时触发零个黑盒检测器并保持水印完整性；该漏洞在三种水印方案和三种模型架构上普遍存在；软件级防御（包括密码学安全伪随机数生成器）完全无效，QRNG+TEE硬件隔离实现100%攻击阻断。

Conclusion: 提出四项监管修正：高风险金融AI系统强制QRNG认证（符合NIST SP 800-90B标准）、推理层供应链审计及输出溯源机制。

Abstract: AI systems are increasingly deployed for credit assessment and investment advisory in global financial markets, yet the integrity of their inference pipelines remains insufficiently addressed by existing regulatory frameworks. This paper identifies and empirically validates an invisible manipulation channel operating at the sampling layer of LLM inference--a vulnerability that allows adversaries to systematically bias AI-generated financial opinions while preserving full compliance with output-based audit mechanisms, including statistical watermarking. We show that this inference-stage manipulation is statistically hard to detect: the Kullback-Leibler divergence between manipulated and normal output distributions can be made arbitrarily small, so that any output-based detection scheme requires impractically large sample sizes to achieve reliable detection power. Empirical experiments across credit rating and investment advisory scenarios show that directional bias keywords can be amplified by 1.8-1.9x under stealth-preserving (aware) manipulation while triggering zero of six black-box detectors and preserving watermark integrity. The vulnerability generalizes across three mainstream watermarking schemes and three heterogeneous model architectures, establishing it as a systemic financial infrastructure risk. Software-based defenses including cryptographically secure pseudorandom number generators are entirely ineffective, while QRNG combined with TEE hardware isolation achieves 100% attack blocking--reducing the target rate to the natural baseline--by replacing the predictable hash key with quantum-derived entropy that renders all pre-computed manipulation targets invalid. We propose four regulatory amendments centered on mandatory QRNG certification for high-risk financial AI systems under NIST SP 800-90B, inference-layer supply chain audits, and output provenance mechanisms.

</details>


### [174] [Scalable Malware Family Classification Using Quantum Kernel Based Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16191)
*Ratun Rahman,Hassan Jalil Hadi,Christopher Gabriel Pedraza Pohlenz,Ali Shoker*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于量子核的机器学习框架（QKML），用于恶意软件家族多分类。该方法通过LDA降维提取结构特征，利用参数化量子电路构建保真度量子核，并采用Nyström近似实现低秩核矩阵，结合岭回归进行分类。在包含18,836个样本、23个恶意软件家族的数据集上达到80.88%的准确率，优于经典机器学习基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 恶意软件家族分类是网络安全的关键挑战，但新兴恶意样本在结构上日益相似且经过混淆，传统机器学习模型在大规模部署时难以实现准确的多类分类。

Method: 提出可扩展的QKML框架：1）从可执行文件中提取结构特征；2）使用监督LDA投影生成紧凑的类感知表示；3）通过参数化量子电路构建基于保真度的量子核；4）采用Nyström近似对量子核进行低秩逼近；5）通过岭回归实现有效的多类分类，避免了核矩阵构造的二次计算成本。

Result: 在包含18,836个样本、23个恶意软件家族的大规模数据集上，模型达到80.88%的准确率，在相同特征和数据划分下优于经典机器学习基线。

Conclusion: 可扩展的量子核机器学习方法能够在真实世界的恶意软件家族分类任务中带来可衡量的性能优势。

Abstract: The classification of malware families is a key challenge in cybersecurity, which enables threat attribution, analysis of attack operations, and the formulation of effective defense strategies. Emerging malware samples are becoming increasingly structurally similar and obfuscated, making accurate multiclass classification challenging for traditional machine learning models, especially when deployed at scale. In this research, we propose a scalable Quantum Kernel-based Machine Learning (QKML) framework for malware family classification that addresses both accuracy and efficiency constraints. The proposed framework extracts structural features from executable files and uses a supervised Linear Discriminant Analysis (LDA) projection to generate a compact, class-aware representation well suited for quantum processing. The nonlinear relationships among malware families are captured using a fidelity-based quantum kernel built from parameterized quantum circuits. We use the Nyström approximation method to obtain a low-rank approximation of the quantum kernel, which enables effective multiclass classification via ridge regression and enables learning from all available training samples without incurring the quadratic computational cost of kernel matrix construction. The proposed model achieves strong classification performance, with 80.88% accuracy, outperforming classical machine learning baselines under identical feature and data splits, according to experimental evaluation on a large-scale malware dataset that includes 18,836 samples across 23 malware families. These findings suggest that scalable quantum-kernel-based machine learning can offer measurable performance advantages for real-world malware family classification tasks.

</details>


### [175] [did:crdt: Coordination-Free Decentralised Identifiers via Signed CRDTs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16223)
*Hugo O'Connor,Claire Barnes*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出 did:crdt，一种基于无冲突复制数据类型（CRDT）的去中心化标识符（DID）方法，无需区块链、排序器或全局总序即可实现 DID 文档的更新与合并，通过 CALM 定理保证状态收敛，并提供了 Rust 参考实现与微秒级合并延迟验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 DID 方法均需要协调机制（如区块链排序、轻量方法无更新能力或 Sidetree 仍需区块链最终确认），导致费用高、延迟大或功能受限。本文旨在消除所有协调需求，实现无需全局总序的 DID 文档更新。

Method: 采用无冲突复制数据类型（CRDT）构建 DID 文档，每个文档字段对应一个独立 CRDT，并发编辑可确定性合并。利用 CALM 定理确保状态合并路径的汇聚性；签名增量路径仅需因果传递（无需全局排序）。提供认证路径（拜占庭容错）与非认证状态合并路径（可信域优化）两种模式。

Result: 给出了数据模型、威胁模型、CRUD 语义、冲突解决策略，以及 Rust 参考实现，通过基于属性的收敛测试验证，合并延迟达到微秒级。

Conclusion: did:crdt 证明了在完全无需协调（无账本、无排序器、无全局总序）的条件下实现符合 W3C DID Core 标准、支持更新的 DID 方法是可行的，为去中心化身份系统提供了新的轻量级架构选择。

Abstract: Existing Decentralised Identifier (DID) methods require coordination, an agreed global order of operations, to update a DID document: blockchain-anchored methods incur fees and latency; lightweight peer methods (did:key, did:peer) offer no update mechanism; and Sidetree methods still require blockchain ordering for finality. We present did:crdt, a DID method that targets W3C DID Core and removes the need for coordination entirely: there is no ledger, no sequencer, and no global total order. Each DID document is composed of signed Conflict-Free Replicated Data Types (CRDTs), one per document field, each chosen so that concurrent edits merge deterministically. By the CALM Theorem, the state-merge path is then confluent: replicas that see the same updates reach the same document in any arrival order. The signed-delta path needs only causal delivery, applying an update after those it builds on, which is far weaker than the total ordering ledgers impose and needs no agreement protocol. We are explicit about scope: every untrusted-peer path is authenticated, so Byzantine fault tolerance (safety even when peers lie or send malformed data) holds for signed deltas and verified-bundle replay, while the unauthenticated state-merge path is a trusted-domain optimisation and key-compromise recovery is bounded by revocation semantics. We give the data and threat model, CRUD semantics, conflict resolution, and a Rust reference implementation with property-based convergence tests and microsecond-scale merge latency.

</details>


### [176] [SPARK: Security Knowledge Priming and Representation-Guided Knowledge Activation for LLM-based Secure Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16244)
*Xiaoyun Xu,Lichao Wu,Jona te Lintelo,Siyu Zhang,Stjepan Picek*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: SPARK是一种推理时安全框架，通过在提示中加入CWE描述和预计算的安全方向token偏置，激活LLM已有的安全知识，无需重新训练即可显著提升代码安全性，在9个开源模型上匹配或超越微调与检索增强方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究认为LLM生成不安全代码是因为缺乏安全知识，导致防御方法依赖繁重的微调或外部知识检索，引入大量计算开销和数据偏差。作者提出相反观点：预训练语料已包含丰富的安全内容，真正的瓶颈在于激活这些已有知识。

Method: SPARK包含两个组件：(I) 检索相关CWE条目并附加简短结构化提示，激活模型已有的安全表示；(II) 在解码时对logits施加预计算的安全方向token偏置，该偏置通过安全/不安全隐藏状态的均值差经语言模型头投影得到，仅需一次离线计算，推理时每个token增加一次向量加法开销。

Result: 在9个开源模型（C++、Java、Python）上评估，与7种基线（微调、检索增强等）对比，SPARK在所有设置下匹配或优于最佳基线，同时保持HumanEval的代码功能性。在Claude、DeepSeek、GPT等7个最强黑盒模型上验证了组件I的有效性。

Conclusion: 不安全代码生成的瓶颈在于安全知识的激活而非缺乏安全知识，SPARK作为轻量级推理时方法能有效激活已有安全表示，为LLM安全对齐提供了一条低成本的替代路径。

Abstract: Large language models routinely generate code with exploitable security flaws. Prior literature attributes this limitation to a lack of security expertise, steering current defense mechanisms toward heavy fine-tuning or external knowledge retrieval, which introduces significant computational overhead and data bias through redundant code examples. Contrary to this view, we argue that pretraining corpora are already rich in security material. The bottleneck is activation: without an explicit and brief cue, statistical pressure toward common training-distribution patterns suppresses the model's safety-relevant representations. We present SPARK, an inference-time security harness that activates this latent knowledge without any retraining. The harness has two parts. Component~I retrieves a few of the relevant Common Weakness Enumeration (CWE) entries for each coding task and appends a short structured cue to the prompt; this alone is enough to surface the model's existing security representations. Component~II adds a precomputed token bias to the logits at every decoding step. We obtain the bias by projecting a safe-direction vector, the unit difference between the mean safe and mean unsafe last-layer hidden states, through the language model head. The bias is computed once offline; applying it costs a single vector addition per generated token. We evaluate SPARK on 9 open-source models across C++, Java, and Python, and compare with 7 baselines spanning fine-tuning and retrieval-augmented methods. SPARK matches or improves on the best baseline in every setting while preserving HumanEval utility. We further test Component~I in a black-box setting on 7 of today's strongest models, including Claude, DeepSeek, and GPT, demonstrating the bottleneck of insecure code generation and the improvements enabled by our method.

</details>


### [177] [Dynamic Malicious Skills in Agentic AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16287)
*Tianhao Chen,Zhengyuan Jiang,Yuepeng Hu,Yebei Gou,Neil Zhenqiang Gong*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文展示了一种针对AI Agent的动态恶意技能攻击：攻击者通过在技能的自然语言文档（如SKILL.md）中嵌入恶意指令，诱使Agent在运行时将恶意逻辑注入原本良性的技能中。作者在OpenHands和Claude Code等框架上验证了该攻击的有效性，并提出了一种基于操作系统内核只读挂载的系统级防御方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 技能（Skills）是AI Agent的关键组件，但引入技能的同时也带来了新的攻击面。作者希望揭示并检验这种通过动态恶意技能进行攻击的安全隐患。

Method: 攻击者将恶意指令嵌入自然语言文档（如SKILL.md），使Agent在执行时动态地将恶意逻辑注入原本良性的技能中。防御方法则采用操作系统内核强制只读挂载，阻止技能的动态修改。

Result: 在OpenHands和Claude Code等Agent框架上，动态恶意技能能够以非平凡的成功率在运行时引入多种恶意行为。提出的只读挂载防御方案能有效阻断此类攻击，且不影响良性技能的正常功能。

Conclusion: 动态恶意技能是Agent系统的一个真实安全威胁，但通过操作系统级别的只读挂载机制可以在系统层面有效防御，展示了安全与功能可兼得的可行性。

Abstract: Skills are a key enabling component of agentic AI. While they enhance agents' capabilities, they also introduce new attack surfaces. In this work, we investigate one such attack surface by demonstrating dynamic malicious skills. By embedding malicious instructions in natural-language documentation (e.g., SKILL.md), an attacker can induce an agent to dynamically inject malicious logic into an otherwise benign skill during execution. We evaluate this attack across agentic frameworks such as OpenHands and Claude Code, showing that dynamic malicious skills can successfully introduce a range of malicious behaviors at runtime with non-trivial success rates. To mitigate this vulnerability, we propose a system-level defense that prevents dynamic modification of skills using operating system kernel-enforced read-only mounts. Our evaluation demonstrates that this defense effectively blocks dynamic malicious skills while preserving the functionality of benign skills.

</details>


### [178] [From Refusal Geometry to Safety Geometry: Harmfulness--Refusal Coupling under Dynamic Adversarial Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16349)
*Wenhao Lan,Shan Li,Xinhua Lai,Meiqi Wu,Junbin Yang,Haihua Shen,Yijun Yang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种双安全-几何协议（dual safety-geometry protocol），用于测量语言模型中的有害性载体（harmfulness carriers）和拒绝载体（refusal carriers）及其耦合程度。通过对齐的指令微调锚点和Mistral-7B-v0.1的SFT/R2D2训练轨迹分析发现：R2D2早期呈现高耦合、强鲁棒性但效用崩溃；后期耦合降低、效用部分恢复但攻击成功率上升；SFT虽亦低耦合但鲁棒性显著更差。研究将有害性-拒绝耦合作为对抗性微调下安全几何动力学的操作性诊断指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全对齐的鲁棒性评估无法区分模型是否学会了识别有害性、激活拒绝策略或耦合这两个过程，作者希望揭示安全对齐的内部几何机制。

Method: 提出双安全-几何协议（dual safety-geometry protocol），测量有害性载体（harmfulness carriers）和拒绝载体（refusal carriers）及其耦合程度。在Mistral-7B-v0.1的SFT和R2D2训练轨迹上进行实验，结合因果干预（causal sweeps）和稀疏GCG/AutoDAN迁移实验进行分析。

Result: 1) 对齐锚点验证了协议：拒绝侧干预比仅有害性干预更能提高攻击成功率，且有害性与拒绝载体近乎正交。2) R2D2早期呈现高耦合、强固定源鲁棒性、饱和安全提示拒绝但效用崩溃；后期耦合降低，效用部分恢复但攻击成功率上升。3) SFT达到低耦合但鲁棒性显著更差，说明低耦合本身不保证安全。4) 因果扫描支持固定协议敏感性，但未建立独立的有害性和拒绝通路。

Conclusion: 有害性-拒绝耦合（H/R coupling）可作为对抗性微调条件下安全几何动态的操作性诊断指标，有助于理解模型安全对齐的内部机制。

Abstract: Safety alignment requires language models to refuse harmful requests without losing the ability to answer benign ones. Existing robustness evaluations, however, do not reveal whether a model has learned to recognize harmfulness, to activate a refusal policy, or to couple these two processes. We study this question with a dual safety-geometry protocol that measures harmfulness carriers, refusal carriers, and their coupling across aligned instruction-tuned anchors and matched Mistral-7B-v0.1 SFT/R2D2 training trajectories. The aligned anchors validate the protocol: refusal-side interventions reopen attack success more strongly than harmfulness-only interventions, while harmfulness and refusal carriers remain nearly orthogonal. Along the Mistral trajectory, R2D2 exhibits a high-coupling early phase with strong fixed-source robustness, saturated safe-prompt refusal, and collapsed benign utility. Later checkpoints move to a lower-coupling regime with partial utility recovery and reopened attack success. SFT provides an important contrast: it also reaches low coupling, but remains substantially less robust, showing that low coupling alone is not a safety guarantee. All-anchor diagnostics and sparse GCG/AutoDAN transfer experiments further show that H/R coupling is informative in the R2D2 regime, whereas SFT transfer is better summarized by drift or behavior-state measures. Causal sweeps support fixed-protocol sensitivity relative to matched unit-direction controls, but do not establish independent harmfulness and refusal pathways. These results frame harmfulness--refusal coupling as an operational diagnostic for safety-geometry dynamics under adversarial fine-tuning.

</details>


### [179] [The Proxy Knows Too Much: Sealing LLM API Routers with Attested TEEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16358)
*Sipeng Xie,Qianhong Wu,Hengrun Lu,Ziliang Sun,Qi Wu,Bo Qin,Qin Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出AEGIS，一种对提供商透明的、经客户端验证的API路由器，通过将明文处理限制在硬件飞地内，防止路由器作为中间人攻击者篡改或窃取LLM交互数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有API路由器作为应用层中间人，可重写工具调用、替换依赖包、进行审计规避攻击和被动窃取机密，而现有客户端防御措施可被绕过。

Method: 将明文处理限制在小型硬件飞地组件中，认证、调度、计费和管理留在不可信主机上；客户端在释放明文前验证飞地；主机无法读取或篡改交互，明文仅流向测量映像指定的目标。

Result: AEGIS阻止了全部四类恶意路由器攻击；可信路径仅851行代码；支持三种提供商原生API；在真实提供商负载下完成所有请求；本地中继开销约6毫秒/请求。

Conclusion: AEGIS有效解决了API路由器作为中间人的安全问题，提供了轻量级、可部署的硬件飞地防护方案。

Abstract: Agents increasingly access large language models (LLMs) through API routers. A router terminates the client's transport-layer security session and opens a separate upstream session, so it holds the full interaction in plaintext. This makes the router an application-layer man-in-the-middle: it can rewrite agent tool calls, swap dependencies for typosquatted packages, trigger attacks only under audit-evading conditions, and passively exfiltrate secrets. Existing client-side defenses are evadable.
  We propose AEGIS, a provider-transparent attested API router whose data path is a client-verified faithful passthrough. AEGISconfines plaintext handling to a small hardware-enclave component while leaving authentication, scheduling, accounting, and management on the untrusted host. The client verifies the enclave before releasing plaintext. The host can neither read nor alter the interaction, and plaintext leaves only toward destinations fixed by the measured image. We show that all four malicious-router attack classes succeed against a plaintext-access baseline and are blocked by AEGIS, including adaptive tests against the same boundary. The trusted path is $851$ lines, carries three provider-native APIs without conversion, and completes every request under real-provider workload and concurrency. In a seeded audit pilot, two commodity coding agents find eight and ten of ten planted invariant violations. The local relay overhead is about six milliseconds per request.

</details>


### [180] [FEnc$^2$: Unifying Data Packing for Efficient Private Inference via Convolution and Architecture-Aware Fragment Encoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16359)
*Ran Ran,Zhaoting Gong,Nuo Xu,Yuanchao Xu,Fan Yao,Wujie Wen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出FEnc2，一个基于片段的CKKS密文编码框架，通过卷积感知编码和架构感知密文压缩，将同态加密推理的旋转操作减少1-2个数量级，相比Orion实现最高228倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有同态加密密文打包策略无法同时兼顾邻近数据元素保留和特征分组，导致密文槽浪费、旋转次数过多和密文数量膨胀，亟需一种统一的编码框架来优化密文布局。

Method: 1) 卷积感知编码（Conv-aware Encoding）：分析选择最优片段大小来解耦空间依赖，联合最小化层间内旋和外旋；2) 架构感知密文压缩（Arch-aware Ct Compression）：在特征/通道缩减层后恢复密文密度，重塑加密工作负载结构。

Result: 在最大批处理下，LeNet/MNIST上相比Orion加速GPU 228.83倍、CPU 226.06倍；MobileNet/ImageNet上加速GPU 4.55倍、CPU 9.43倍。

Conclusion: 应用层数据布局是加密推理的一阶架构设计维度，FEnc2通过优化加密张量布局在互补于底层原语优化的同时大幅降低计算开销，是下一代FHE系统的重要使能技术。

Abstract: Fully Homomorphic Encryption (FHE) enables privacy-preserving machine learning but incurs extreme computational and memory overhead. These costs come not only from expensive low-level primitives, including Number Theoretic Transform (NTT), rotation, and key-switching, but also from inefficient ciphertext packing at the application level. Existing packing strategies typically preserve either neighboring data elements or feature grouping, but not both, leading to wasted ciphertext slots, excessive rotations, and inflated ciphertext counts. We propose FEnc2, a unified and principled fragment-based encoding framework for CKKS-based private convolutional neural network inference. FEnc2 optimizes slot utilization, rotation complexity, and ciphertext density through two components: 1)Conv-aware Encoding, which analytically selects an optimal fragment size to decouple spatial dependencies and jointly minimize inner-outer rotations across layers, and 2)Arch-aware Ct Compression, which restores ciphertext density after feature- or channel-reduction layers. Together, these transformations reshape encrypted workload structure and reduce homomorphic operations by one to two orders of magnitude. With full memory capacity utilized, i.e., at maximum batch size, FEnc2 achieves end-to-end latency speedups over the state-of-the-art Orion of up to 228.83x on GPU and 226.06x on CPU for LeNet on MNIST, and up to 4.55x on GPU and 9.43x on CPU for MobileNet on ImageNet. FEnc2 is hardware-agnostic yet architecturally transformative: by optimizing encrypted tensor layout before execution, it reduces ciphertext count and workload pressure on hardware, complementing primitive-level optimizations such as NTT and keyswitch accelerators. These results show that application-level data layout is a first-order architectural design dimension for encrypted inference and an important enabler for next-generation FHE systems.

</details>


### [181] [MIPSBLEED: Uncovering Microarchitectural Timing Leaks in Pervasive Embedded Processors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16372)
*Ahmed Najeeb,Billy Bob Brumley*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了MIPSBLEED框架，首次系统性地揭示了MIPS处理器在启用SMT（同步多线程）后，L1数据缓存、L1指令缓存和执行引擎三个共享微架构组件存在跨核时序侧信道信息泄露，并在真实椭圆曲线密码工具包上实现了单迹线密钥恢复攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MIPS处理器在路由器、工业控制器和物联网系统中广泛使用，但其针对现代侧信道攻击的安全性研究严重不足。SMT已被用于提升这些平台性能，但其引入的跨核时序侧信道风险尚未被系统评估。

Method: 构建MIPSBLEED分析框架，针对L1数据缓存、L1指令缓存和执行引擎三个共享微架构组件，设计汇编级探针进行定量泄漏评估，无需特权权限即可实施高分辨率时序攻击。

Result: 三个共享通道均存在显著的信息泄漏，并在真实椭圆曲线密码（ECC）工具包上实现了单迹线密钥恢复攻击。

Conclusion: MIPS是一个被忽视但关键的微架构安全目标，亟需在资源受限且启用SMT的嵌入式系统中部署轻量级隔离机制。

Abstract: Despite their age, MIPS processors remain deeply embedded in routers, industrial controllers, and IoT systems, yet their security against modern side-channel attacks has received little attention. This paper exposes how Simultaneous Multithreading (SMT), a feature increasingly used to boost performance in these environments, creates powerful cross-core timing channels on MIPS-based platforms. We introduce MIPSBLEED, a systematic analysis and exploitation framework that uncovers leakage in three shared microarchitectural components: the L1 data cache, L1 instruction cache, and the execution engine. Through carefully crafted assembly-level probes and quantitative leakage assessment, we demonstrate practical, high-resolution timing attacks that operate without requiring privileged access. Our evaluation reveals significant information leakage across all three channels and culminates in a single trace key recovery attack on a real elliptic curve cryptographic toolkit. These results position MIPS as an overlooked yet critical target in the study of microarchitectural security and underscore the urgent need for lightweight isolation mechanisms in resource-constrained, SMT-enabled embedded systems.

</details>


### [182] [MPX: A Unified Systolic Array for Matrix and Polynomial Multiplication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16394)
*George Alexakis,Dimitrios Schoinianakis,Giorgos Dimitrakopoulos*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出MPX，一种支持矩阵乘法和直接多项式乘法的双模式脉动阵列，在传统脉动阵列基础上仅增加20%面积开销，即可实现比基于NTT的多项式乘法低1.2倍以上的延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作倾向于将NTT计算映射到现有的脉动矩阵引擎上，以复用AI硬件进行密码学加速，但这种方法并非最优。本文采取相反思路，利用脉动阵列的波前数据流与多项式乘法累加模式之间的自然对齐关系，设计统一硬件架构。

Method: 提出MPX双模式脉动阵列，支持两种工作模式：矩阵乘法模式和直接多项式乘法模式。通过利用波前数据流与多项式乘法累加模式的对应关系，在相同硬件结构上实现两种运算的直接支持。

Result: 在传统脉动阵列基础上仅增加20%面积开销，且在多项式乘法模式下，相比基于NTT的脉动矩阵引擎方案延迟降低1.2倍以上。矩阵乘法执行时的功耗开销可忽略不计。

Conclusion: MPX证明了在通用脉动阵列上以较低硬件代价同时高效支持矩阵乘法和多项式乘法的可行性，为密码学工作负载与AI工作负载的硬件复用提供了新思路。

Abstract: Polynomial multiplication is a fundamental kernel in Fully Homomorphic Encryption (FHE) and post-quantum cryptography (PQC) and is commonly accelerated through Number Theoretic Transforms (NTTs). To avoid the cost of designing dedicated cryptographic accelerators, recent efforts have mapped NTT computations onto existing systolic matrix engines, enabling the reuse of AI hardware for cryptographic workloads. In this work, we take the opposite approach.
