We observe that the wavefront dataflow of systolic arrays naturally aligns with the accumulation pattern of polynomial multiplication and leverage this correspondence to design MPX, a dual-mode systolic array that supports both matrix multiplication and direct polynomial multiplication within the same hardware fabric. Experimental results show that extending a conventional systolic array with this dual-mode capability requires only 20% additional area and introduces negligible power overhead during matrix-multiplication execution. In polynomial-multiplication mode, MPX achieves more than 1.2x lower latency compared to NTT-based polynomial multiplication on systolic matrix engines.

</details>


### [183] [Transferable Self-Evolving Playbooks for Agentic Security Auditing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16420)
*Ziyue Wang,Cheuk Wang Maurice Ng,Chenchen Yu,Strick Sheng,Kaihua Qin,Liyi Zhou*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: EvoHunt 是一个基于开源仓库的审计剧本进化环境，通过自动化剧本演化（而非人工策划）来提升LLM代理的漏洞发现与验证能力。实验表明，进化后的剧本不仅超越了商业产品（OpenAI Codex Security），还能将能力迁移到更弱的模型上（如Qwen3.6-27B提升2.5倍以上）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM代理用于漏洞发现时依赖人工编写的审计剧本（提示工程、工作流、知识库、启发式规则等），这引发了两个核心问题：1）剧本创建能否自动化而非人工策展？2）进化后的剧本能否将审计过程迁移到更弱的代理上，提升其能力？

Method: EvoHunt 提出一个剧本进化循环，包含三个代理：审计代理（执行当前剧本并产生发现）、评估器（根据真实标签评分）、修订器（基于失败分析更新剧本）。剧本格式无约束，可从空白开始添加或移除工作流、启发式规则、漏洞知识等。进化后的剧本只需少量适配即可在不同LLM或框架下运行。

Result: 1）剧本进化使Codex/GPT5.4-xhigh的端到端利用成功率从1.1%提升至6.2%（6倍提升）；2）进化后的OpenCode/GLM5.1剧本在所有指标上超越OpenAI Codex Security，目标匹配率达11.3% vs 9.2%；3）迁移方面，GLM进化的剧本使Qwen3.6-27B从2.4%提升至6.5%，Qwen3.6-35B-A3B从1.1%提升至4.6%，A3B获得比GPT迁移多2.4倍的匹配数。

Conclusion: EvoHunt验证了自动化剧本进化的可行性：开源进化可以超越商业产品，且进化后的审计知识可以有效迁移到更弱的模型，实现能力普惠。

Abstract: An LLM agent for vulnerability discovery and validation is more than a model. It combines three components: an LLM for code analysis, an agent harness such as Codex or OpenCode for navigation, tool use, and execution, and an audit playbook, domain-specific procedural knowledge that guides the LLM and harness toward vulnerability discovery. Prior work relies on human-supplied playbooks, including prompt engineering, manual workflows, knowledge bases, and heuristics. This raises two research questions: Acquisition - is human curation necessary, and can playbook creation be automated? Transfer - can an evolved playbook transfer the audit procedure to weaker agents, improving their capability?
  We present EvoHunt, a playbook evolution environment over open-source repositories for security auditing. Three agents drive the evolution loop: an audit agent rolls out the current playbook and produces findings; an evaluator scores outcomes against ground truth; and a reviser commits updates to the playbook based on failure analysis. The playbook format is unconstrained: starting empty, EvoHunt adds or removes workflows, heuristics, vulnerability knowledge, or domain-specific content. The evolved playbook requires only minor adaptation to run under a different LLM or harness.
  We evaluate EvoHunt on open-source security advisories. For acquisition, playbook evolution raises end-to-end exploits for Codex/GPT5.4-xhigh 6x, from 1.1% to 6.2%, and the evolved OpenCode/GLM5.1 playbook surpasses OpenAI Codex Security on every metric, with 11.3% vs. 9.2% target-match rate, showing open-source evolution can outperform a dedicated commercial product. For transfer, the GLM-evolved playbook gives the strongest student lift: Qwen3.6-27B improves from 2.4% to 6.5%, Qwen3.6-35B-A3B from 1.1% to 4.6%, and A3B obtains 2.4x more matches than GPT transfer.

</details>


### [184] [A Formal Resilience Framework for Cyber-Physical Embodied Systems under Device-Level Cyberattacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16467)
*Alberto Giaretta*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种形式化的可信赖框架，将入侵检测系统（IDS）信息融入弹性评估谓词，使具身信息物理系统（CPS）能够主动评估网络攻击对任务执行和实体保全的影响，并判断是否需要部署缓解策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统CPS容错方法基于传感器/执行器输出分析来检测渐进漂移或突发故障，但无法捕捉网络攻击的微妙策略。在具身CPS中，计算与物理设备不仅参与任务完成，还负责实体保全（维持系统物理完整性），因此需要能够主动应对网络攻击的框架。

Method: 提出一个形式化可信赖框架，将IDS信息整合到弹性评估谓词中，支持对网络攻击如何影响任务执行和实体保全进行结构化推理，并判断是否需要部署缓解策略。

Result: 通过分析性示例证明了该框架的分析能力和合理性，为可信赖且安全的具身CPS奠定了理论基础。

Conclusion: 该框架使具身CPS能够主动响应网络攻击，评估系统对干扰和退化的容忍度，从而防止结构性物理损坏，弥补了传统容错方法在网络攻击场景下的不足。

Abstract: In cyber-physical systems (CPSs), fault tolerance is traditionally achieved by analysing sensor and actuator outputs, detecting progressive drift or sudden failures, and initiating suitable tolerance mechanisms. Reasonable under general failure models, this approach fails to capture nuanced disruptions caused by cyberattacks, which may employ subtle strategies. This is particularly critical in embodied CPSs, where computational and physical devices not only have an active role in task completion, but also in embodiment preservation (that is, maintaining the system's physical integrity). To prevent structural physical damage, embodied CPSs require a framework that enables proactive response to cyberattacks. This paper proposes a formal dependability framework that incorporates IDS information into resilience evaluation predicates, enabling assessment of tolerance to disruption and degradation. The framework supports structured reasoning about how cyberattacks affect task execution and embodiment preservation, and whether mitigation strategies must be deployed. Analytical examples demonstrate its analytical capability and soundness, establishing a theoretical foundation for dependable and secure embodied CPSs.

</details>


### [185] [Measurement Study of Post-Quantum Readiness of Internet: 2026](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16473)
*Vanishka Mohan Dubey,Gaurav Varshney*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文对32,011个域名的TLS部署进行大规模实证评估，发现尽管49.3%的域名支持混合后量子密钥交换（如MLKEM768+X25519），但仍有50.7%使用经典密钥交换，且混合后量子证书采用率为0%，表明互联网通信系统在认证层面仍易受量子攻击威胁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子计算的出现对当前依赖RSA和ECC等经典公钥算法的TLS安全体系构成根本性挑战，亟需评估现实世界中后量子密码的迁移准备情况。

Method: 对32,011个域名进行大规模实证评估，分析不同行业的TLS协商参数，包括协议版本、密码套件、密钥交换机制和证书类型。

Result: 15.70%的域（特别是银行和政府等关键行业）仍使用TLS 1.2；49.3%支持混合后量子密钥交换，50.7%仍用经典密钥交换；混合后量子证书采用率为0%。

Conclusion: 实现完全的量子韧性需要密钥交换与证书基础设施的协同迁移，否则互联网通信系统仍面临Harvest-Now-Decrypt-Later（HNDL）等长期威胁。

Abstract: The emergence of quantum computing presents a fundamental challenge to the security of current Internet communication systems. Transport Layer Security (TLS), which forms the backbone of secure web communication, predominantly relies on classical public-key cryptographic algorithms such as RSA and elliptic curve cryptography (ECC), both of which are susceptible to quantum attacks. This paper conducts a large scale empirical evaluation of post-quantum readiness across 32,011 domains, with a primary focus on real-world TLS deployments across diverse sectors by analysing negotiated TLS parameters, including protocol versions, cipher suites, key exchange mechanisms, and certificates. The results indicate that while modern protocols like TLS 1.3 and QUIC are gaining adoption, 15.70% of domains especially in critical sectors such as banking and government still rely on TLS 1.2.
  Furthermore, 49.3% of domains support hybrid post-quantum key exchange mechanisms (e.g., MLKEM768 with X25519), whereas 50.7% continue to use classical key exchange, reflecting partial transition. Notably, 0% adoption of hybrid post-quantum certificates was observed, leaving the authentication layer vulnerable to quantum-enabled attacks such as certificate forgery. The findings reveal uneven adoption of post-quantum mechanisms across sectors, where technology driven platforms are advancing more rapidly than legacy-dependent infrastructures. Overall, the study highlights that achieving complete quantum resilience requires a coordinated transition not only in key exchange mechanisms but also in certificate infrastructures. Without such comprehensive migration, Internet communication systems remain vulnerable to long-term threats, including Harvest-Now-Decrypt-Later (HNDL) attacks.

</details>


### [186] [DoubtProbe: Black-Box Jailbreak Defense via Structural Verification and Semantic Auditing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16527)
*Xuanyu Yin,Yilin Jiang,Jun Zhou,Kai Chen,Zhengfu Cao,Xiaolei Dong*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出DoubtProbe，一种双分支推理时防御框架，结合结构验证与语义审计，通过检测原始请求与重构请求之间的信息保持失败来防御黑盒越狱攻击。在Qwen2.5-72B上，JBB攻击成功率从0.293降至0.100，CodeAttack从0.152降至0.001，假阳性率仅0.016-0.022。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有黑盒越狱防御方法在面对不断演变的提示包装、表达式重写和结构操纵时变得不稳定。观察到许多黑盒越狱并未移除有害目标，只是重组了表达和执行所需的信息，从而在生成过程中仍可恢复。

Method: DoubtProbe包含两个分支：(1) 结构分支：从原始请求提取结构化表示，在表示约束下重构请求，检测原始与重构请求之间的信息保持失败；(2) 语义分支：直接审计原始提示。将黑盒越狱防御形式化为受控变换下的一致性检查。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-72B上，JBB攻击成功率从0.293降至0.100，CodeAttack从0.152降至0.001；AlpacaEval上假阳性率0.022，OR-Bench上0.016。在Llama-3.1-70B上保持稳定，展示了良好的骨干迁移性。

Conclusion: 结构不一致信号为黑盒越狱防御提供了实用且可泛化的基础，尤其当与语义审计结合时，能够实现更强且更稳定的防御-效用权衡。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in user-facing systems, black-box jailbreak defense has become an important practical problem. Existing defenses often rely on known-attack coverage, prompt-level semantic judgment, or local runtime control, yet these paths can become unstable under evolving prompt packaging, expression rewriting, and structure manipulation. We observe that many black-box jailbreaks do not remove the harmful goal, but reorganize the information needed to express and execute it, thereby evading safety alignment while remaining recoverable during generation. Motivated by this observation, we propose DoubtProbe, a dual-branch inference-time defense framework that combines structural verification with semantic auditing and formulates black-box jailbreak defense as consistency checking under controlled transformation. The structural branch extracts a structured representation from the original request, reconstructs the request under representation constraints, and detects information-preservation failures between the original and reconstructed requests; the semantic branch audits the original prompt directly. We evaluate DoubtProbe against representative black-box defenses on jailbreak and benign-request benchmarks, and further test backbone transfer from Qwen2.5-72B to Llama-3.1-70B. Results show that DoubtProbe achieves a stronger and more stable defense-utility trade-off: on Qwen2.5-72B, it reduces the JBB attack success rate from 0.293 to 0.100 and the CodeAttack attack success rate from 0.152 to 0.001, while maintaining false positive rates of 0.022 and 0.016 on AlpacaEval and OR-Bench; the same pattern remains stable on Llama-3.1-70B. These findings show that structural inconsistency signals provide a practical and generalizable basis for black-box jailbreak defense, especially when combined with semantic auditing.

</details>


### [187] [A data-driven security quantification framework for IoT-based systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16561)
*Alhassan Abdulhamid,Sohag Kabir,Ibrahim Ghafir,Ci Lei*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种数据驱动的概率安全框架，通过集成MBSE、攻击树分析和EPSS漏洞评分系统，为IoT安全关键系统提供了可复现的量化风险评估方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IoT环境中的定量网络安全评估面临异构系统架构、不断演变的威胁景观以及缺乏可靠概率可利用性数据的挑战，传统ATA量化依赖主观专家判断，引入不确定性并降低分析可复现性。

Method: 利用SysML模型捕获系统架构并导出攻击树；将漏洞映射为基本攻击步骤并使用EPSS分配可利用概率；将攻击树表示为贝叶斯网络，实现概率推理、诊断推断和漏洞关键性分析。

Result: 该框架能够量化系统被攻破的概率，识别攻击的可能原因，并优先确定缓解策略。

Conclusion: 通过将架构驱动建模与真实漏洞情报相结合，该框架为复杂IoT环境中的网络安全风险评估提供了一种严谨、可复现的方法。

Abstract: The Internet of Things (IoT) is integral to modern cyber-physical systems. Quantitative cybersecurity assessment in IoT environments remains challenging due to heterogeneous system architectures, evolving threat landscapes, and the limited availability of reliable probabilistic exploitability data. Although Attack Tree Analysis (ATA) provides a structured framework for modelling potential attack paths leading to system compromise, conventional ATA quantification often relies on subjective expert judgement or heuristic scoring schemes, which can introduce uncertainty and reduce analytical reproducibility. This study introduces a data-driven probabilistic security framework for IoT-based safety-critical systems by integrating Model-Based Systems Engineering (MBSE), ATA, and empirical vulnerability data. In the proposed framework, SysML models capture system architecture, from which attack trees are derived. Vulnerabilities are mapped as Basic Attack Steps and assigned exploitation probabilities using the Exploit Prediction Scoring System (EPSS). The attack tree is then represented as a Bayesian Network, enabling probabilistic reasoning, diagnostic inference, and vulnerability criticality analysis. The framework quantifies system compromise probabilities, identifies likely causes of attacks, and prioritises mitigation strategies. By combining architecture-driven modelling with real-world vulnerability intelligence, it provides a rigorous, reproducible approach for cybersecurity risk assessment in complex IoT environments.

</details>


### [188] [SoK: Taxonomizing the Low-Level Attack Surface of Modern Web Browsers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16646)
*Han Zheng,Qinying Wang,Qiang Liu,Mathias Payer*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文通过对2016-2025年间2233个内存损坏漏洞的系统化分析，构建了基于输入×组件×权限的浏览器攻击面分类法，揭示了当前浏览器安全测试主要集中于已充分探索的组件，而漏洞密集、高影响力的攻击面仍测试不足，并识别出三个与学术努力正交的模糊测试部署缺口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管浏览器测试和漏洞披露工作已开展十年，社区仍缺乏面向防御的低级攻击面系统化研究。现有SoK对漏洞和缓解技术的调查较为分散，未回答现代浏览器低级攻击面如何结构化、哪些部分尚未被安全测试充分探索这一核心问题。

Method: 提出三个子问题：(RQ1)基于架构推导输入×组件×权限分类法；(RQ2)将2016-2025年间的2233个内存损坏报告映射到该分类法；(RQ3)将十年间学术浏览器模糊测试器按输入类覆盖到漏洞密度图上，分析测试覆盖缺口。

Result: 当前测试集中于已充分探索的组件，而漏洞密集、高影响力的攻击面测试不足；识别出三个与学术努力正交的模糊测试部署缺口。

Conclusion: 为未来浏览器安全研究提供了结构化的基础框架，指明了安全测试资源应优先投入的方向。

Abstract: The web browser remains one of the most exposed remote attack surfaces on end-user systems, and memory-corruption flaws continue to play a central role in real-world browser exploitation. Despite a decade of intensive browser testing and bug-disclosure efforts, the community still lacks an explicit, defense-oriented systematization of the browser's low-level attack surface. Prior SoKs have surveyed browser vulnerabilities and mitigation techniques. However, these perspectives remain fragmented, leaving open a central question: how is the low-level attack surface of modern web browsers structured, and which parts of this surface remain underexplored by existing security testing?
  We approach this primary question through three sub-questions. (RQ1) How is the browser's attack surface structured along input classes and components? (RQ2) Where do memory corruption vulnerabilities arise within this taxonomy? (RQ3) What do these attack-surface patterns imply for existing browser security testing? To answer RQ1, we derive an architecture-grounded Input x Component x Privilege taxonomy that abstracts the architectures of browsers into a unified view. To answer RQ2, we map 2,233 memory corruption reports disclosed between 2016 and 2025 onto this taxonomy. To answer RQ3, we overlay a decade of academic browser fuzzers, classified by the targeted input class, onto the bug-density map. Our systematization reveals that current testing concentrates on well-explored components while bug-dense, high-impact surfaces remain insufficiently tested. Moreover, we identify three fuzzer deployment gaps, which are orthogonal to the academic efforts. Our work offers a structured foundation for future browser security research.

</details>


### [189] [From Third-Party to First-Party: Measuring and Protecting Against Modern Web Tracking Mechanisms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16720)
*Christian Böttger,Tareq Khouja,Norbert Pohlmann,Nurullah Demir,Tobias Urban*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文对主流网站进行大规模测量，发现超过54%的网站已部署第一方追踪(FPT)或服务器端追踪(SST)。通过脚本聚类和网络图分析，揭示该生态系统由Google等主要厂商主导且高度互联。研究发现现有过滤列表对第一方追踪基本无效，并提出新规则可多拦截63%的追踪请求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Web追踪正从第三方追踪转向第一方追踪和服务器端追踪，但现有检测工具对此类新机制效果不佳，且缺乏大规模部署情况的系统性理解及通用防护机制。

Method: 对主流网站进行大规模测量；开发不依赖特定供应商的检测方法；基于脚本相似性进行聚类并构建网络图分析生态系统结构；评估现有过滤列表效果并提出新规则。

Result: 超过54%的网站部署了FPT或SST相关技术；生态系统由Google等主要厂商主导且高度互联；现有过滤列表对第一方追踪基本无效；提出的新规则可多拦截63%的追踪请求。

Conclusion: 第一方追踪和服务器端追踪已广泛普及，现有防护手段不足，需开发新的检测和防御机制以应对这一趋势。

Abstract: Web user tracking has always been a cat-and-mouse game between privacy-conscious users and trackers. Recently, this conflict has driven a shift from third-party tracking toward first-party tracking (FPT) and server-side tracking (SST). By relocating tracking logic to the browser's first-party context or the website's backend, these mechanisms obscure data flows and render traditional client-side detection tools increasingly ineffective. Despite the growing adoption of these techniques, our understanding of their deployment at scale remains limited, and generalized protection mechanisms are lacking.
  In this work, we conduct a large-scale measurement of top sites to assess this shift and the prevalence of FPT and SST. We develop a provider-independent methodology to detect these mechanisms and find that over 54% of analyzed sites now deploy FPT or SST-related techniques. By clustering scripts based on their similarity and constructing a network graph, we demonstrate that the ecosystem is densely connected and dominated by major vendors like Google. Finally, we demonstrate that current filter lists are largely ineffective against first-party tracking, and we propose new rules to address this gap. We show that these rules block 63% more requests than traditional filter lists.

</details>


### [190] [Automated jailbreak attack targeting multiple defense strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16751)
*Qi Wang,Chengcheng Wan,Weijia He,Yanqing Li,Hanqi Sun,Xiaodong Gu,Jiangtao Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出UNIATTACK，一个从防御视角构建的黑盒对抗测试框架，通过提取高影响力攻击特征并利用专用攻击LLM优化，生成跨模型、跨类别的单次攻击模板，相比基线方法攻击成功率提升64.63%-248.82%，成本仅为其0.03%-4.96%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在广泛任务中展现出强大能力，但其安全性因易受对抗性提示攻击而成为关键问题。现有方法依赖静态模板或迭代模型特定调优，缺乏高效且通用的攻击测试工具。

Method: UNIATTACK从现有多种攻击中提取最小但高影响力的攻击特征，通过专用攻击LLM进行优化，并经过自动化精炼过程将其组合成灵活模板，实现基于特征构建的单次黑盒攻击。

Result: 在部署多层防御机制的模型上，相比基线方法，UNIATTACK平均攻击成功率提升64.63%-248.82%，而成本仅占基线的0.03%-4.96%。

Conclusion: UNIATTACK提供了一种高效、低成本且跨模型泛化的黑盒对抗测试框架，可作为评估LLM鲁棒性的实用工具。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable capabilities across a wide range of tasks. However, their safety remains a critical concern due to their susceptibility to adversarial prompt-based attacks. In this paper, we present UNIATTACK, an adversarial testing framework designed from a defense-oriented perspective to systematically construct effective black-box attack prompts. Unlike prior approaches that rely on static templates or iterative model-specific tuning, UNIATTACK extracts minimal but high-impact attack features from diverse existing attacks, optimizes them via a specialized attacker LLM, and composes them into flexible templates through automated refinement process. This feature-centric construction enables one-shot attacks that generalize across multiple models and safety categories, providing a practical tool for assessing LLM robustness. Our evaluation results shows that compared to the baselines, UNIATTACK achieves an average attack success rate (ASR) improvement of 64.63\%-248.82\% on models deployed with multi-layered defense mechanisms and it only takes 0.03\%-4.96\% cost of the baselines. UNIATTACK artifact is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/UniAttack-Artifact-30F1.

</details>


### [191] [Cross-Silo De-Anonymization Under Local Differential Privacy: Threat Model, Phase Transition, and Coordination Necessity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16763)
*Ziniu Liu,Aiping Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出跨数据孤岛的人员级差分隐私（XSP-DP）框架，证明去匿名化在 k* = Θ(log n / ε²) 处存在相变：低于阈值时攻击失败，高于阈值时攻击必然成功，且非协调的本地随机响应机制无法避免此威胁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准组合差分隐私给出的最坏情况保证 (kε, kδ) 无法回答实际问题：当一个人的记录出现在 k 个独立数据孤岛中时，对手在什么条件下能成功识别目标个体？需要建立信息论框架来精确刻画这一去匿名化阈值。

Method: 提出跨孤岛人员级DP（XSP-DP）的Pufferfish风格隐私定义，将单个人员在所有孤岛上的全部记录定义为邻接关系；利用Fano不等式推导去匿名化的下界，构造最大似然估计器证明上界，并通过XOR+随机响应构造展示信息协同效应。

Result: 证明了去匿名化在 k* = Θ(log n / ε²) 处存在相变；每个孤岛输出单独无信息时，联合互信息仍严格为正；非协调的二元随机响应机制下，一旦k超过阈值，去匿名化不可避免。

Conclusion: 建立了跨孤岛推理攻击的基线威胁模型和Θ级别的阈值，表明跨孤岛协调对于防御是必要的。

Abstract: When a person's records appear in k independent data silos, each protected by (epsilon, delta)-differential privacy, standard composition yields a valid (k*epsilon, k*delta)-DP guarantee for the joint output. This worst-case bound, however, does not answer the concrete inference question: at what k can an adversary actually identify a target person? This paper develops the information-theoretic framework needed to answer that question.
  We introduce cross-silo person-level DP (XSP-DP), a Pufferfish-style privacy notion whose adjacency relation captures all records of a single person across all silos simultaneously, and verify that the standard basic composition bound carries over to this adjacency model. Within this framework we prove that de-anonymization undergoes a phase transition at k* = Theta(log n / epsilon^2) (population size n, per-silo RR parameter epsilon): a Fano lower bound shows any estimator fails for k << k*, while a matching maximum-likelihood upper bound shows the attack succeeds for k >> k*. An explicit XOR + randomized-response construction demonstrates information synergy: each silo's output is individually uninformative about the target, yet the joint mutual information is strictly positive. For non-coordinated binary randomized-response mechanisms, we prove that de-anonymization is inevitable once k exceeds the threshold, establishing that cross-silo coordination is necessary.
  These results provide a baseline threat model and Theta-level threshold for cross-silo inference attacks under local DP.

</details>


### [192] [The Ghosts of Polymarket: When Off-Chain Matches Meet On-Chain Reverts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16852)
*Yiming Shen,Yuhan Jin,Shuohan Wu,Yanlin Wang,Jiachi Chen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究Polymarket预测市场中因混合架构（链下匹配+链上结算）导致的“Ghost Fills”问题，即订单在链下匹配成功但链上结算失败。通过构建GHOSTHUNTER工具，发现攻击者可利用匹配与结算间的时间差实施四种攻击向量（nonce bump、balance drain、allowance revoke、proxy trap），导致980,133个订单被选择性回滚，造成至少149万美元利润、17.8亿美元资金风险以及实际DoS攻击。该漏洞已影响10条链上的167个合约，涉及至少2300万美元用户资金。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Polymarket的混合架构（链下匹配+链上结算）存在一致性漏洞：订单在链下成功匹配后，可能在链上结算时因攻击者操纵而失败。这一设计缺陷（Ghost Fills）可能导致安全风险和经济损失，需要系统性地研究其影响范围和攻击模式。

Method: 构建GHOSTHUNTER工具，通过重构1,952,440笔回滚的match-order交易，从链上交易追踪中还原失败的结算过程，并将失败原因归因到具体的攻击模式。

Result: 发现四种攻击向量（nonce bump、balance drain、allowance revoke、proxy trap）的35个变种，导致980,133个订单被选择性回滚。攻击者实现至少149万美元利润，17.8亿美元资金面临风险，运营商支付217万POL（约21.2万美元）。高峰期超过24.3%的已填订单被回滚，造成事实上的DoS攻击。该漏洞代码还出现在10条链的167个独立合约中，涉及至少2300万美元用户资金。

Conclusion: Polymarket的混合架构存在严重的安全漏洞，攻击者可以利用链下与链上执行的时间差操纵订单结算。该问题已向受影响方披露并得到部分缓解，但其影响范围已远超Polymarket本身，扩展到多个链上的衍生合约。

Abstract: Polymarket has emerged as a prominent prediction market platform and one of the fastest-growing applications in DeFi. To achieve low-latency trading, it adopts a hybrid architecture that matches orders off-chain but settles them on-chain for final execution. This design creates a consistency gap we call Ghost Fills: an order that is successfully matched off-chain may later fail during on-chain settlement. To understand the security implications of this gap, we investigate such failed settlements by building GHOSTHUNTER, which reconstructs them from on-chain traces and attributes to concrete attack patterns. Across 1,952,440 reverted match-order transactions, we find that attackers exploit the time gap between matching and settlement to invalidate already matched orders before they are finalized on-chain. We then identify four attack vectors from these incidents: nonce bump, balance drain, allowance revoke, and proxy trap, realized via 35 evolving variants. These vectors allow attackers to selectively revert 980,133 filled orders, enabling risk-free prediction, arbitrage-bot hunting, and liquidity reward manipulation, realizing at least \$1.49M in profit, which places \$1.78 B USD at risk and 2.17 M POL (about \$212 K) paid by operator. During peak hours, more than 24.3% of all filled orders reverted, causing de facto DoS attacks. We also find that code derived from the flawed contract still appears in 167 independent contracts across 10 chains holding at least \$23 M in user funds, extending the impact beyond Polymarket. We have disclosed our evidence to affected parties, and the issue has been partially mitigated.

</details>


### [193] [Di5Guise: 5G Privacy with vSIM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.16943)
*Shirin Ebadi,Zach Moolman,Eric Keller,Tamara Lehman*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出Di5Guise架构，通过vSIM虚拟化SIM卡解耦设备身份与用户身份，打破eSIM固定绑定导致的身份关联，将用户重识别准确率从93%降至49%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有eSIM存在固定设备配置（含密钥、证书、唯一eUICC标识），永久绑定每个用户配置到同一设备，使攻击者可通过运营商视角关联用户身份，获取完整活动轨迹、移动模式和真实身份，即使轮换用户身份或使用流量混淆也无效。

Method: 提出Di5Guise架构，核心是vSIM（虚拟化SIM卡），支持动态设备配置供应，使每个用户配置绑定不同的、不可关联的设备配置；通过安全硬件确保vSIM运行在可信状态以建立信任。

Result: 在FPGA板卡上原型实现，集成srsRAN验证与现有5G基础设施完全兼容；使用复杂用户关联模型测试，将用户重识别准确率从93%降至49%（结合混淆技术）。

Conclusion: Di5Guise通过解耦设备身份与用户身份，从根本上打破了eSIM的固定绑定所导致的隐私泄露问题，显著提升了用户隐私保护能力。

Abstract: SIM cards have been the key building block of user authenticationand security in cellular networks. While they are meant to serve as privacy protecting elements in cellular communications, they can be the root cause of privacy loss. Current eSIMs come with a fixed device profile--comprising a secret key, a certificate, and a unique eUICC identifier--that permanently binds every subscriber profile provisioned on the device to that device profile. This binding enables an attacker with the vantage point of a cellular operator to correlate subscriber identities back to a single device, piecing together a complete pattern of life--online activities, movement patterns, and real-world identity--even when users rotate subscriber identities or employ traffic obfuscation techniques. To mitigate this concern, we introduce Di5Guise, a privacy-enhancing architecture that breaks this correlation at its root by decoupling the device identity from the subscriber identity. Central to Di5Guise is vSIM, a virtualized SIM card that enables dynamic device profile provisioning, allowing each subscriber profile to be associated with a distinct, unlinkable device profile. Di5Guise establishes trust with the operator by ensuring that vSIM is running on secure hardware in a trustworthy state. We prototype Di5Guise on a Field Programmable Gate Array (FPGA) board and integrate it with srsRAN to demonstrate full compatibility with existing 5G infrastructure. Using a complex user correlation model, we show that Di5Guise reduces user re-identification accuracy from 93% to 49% when combined with obfuscation.

</details>


### [194] [Phishing Email Detection Using Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.10104)
*Najmul Hasan,Prashanth BusiReddyGari,Haitao Zhao,Yihao Ren,Jinsheng Xu,Shaohu Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出LLMPEA框架，利用大语言模型（GPT-4o、Claude Sonnet 4、Grok-3）检测多向量钓鱼邮件攻击（提示注入、文本改写、多语言攻击），实验显示检测准确率超90%，但也揭示LLM系统易受对抗攻击、提示注入和多语言攻击利用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子邮件钓鱼是最普遍且影响最严重的网络入侵载体之一。随着系统越来越多地部署大语言模型应用，这些系统面临利用其基础架构的不断演变的钓鱼邮件威胁。当前的LLM在部署到邮件安全系统前需要大量加固，尤其是针对利用架构漏洞的协调多向量攻击。

Method: 提出LLMPEA框架，基于LLM检测多向量钓鱼邮件攻击（提示注入、文本改写、多语言攻击）。评估三种前沿LLM（GPT-4o、Claude Sonnet 4、Grok-3）并结合全面提示设计，对其可行性、鲁棒性和局限性进行实证分析。

Result: LLM检测钓鱼邮件的准确率超过90%；但LLM-based钓鱼邮件检测系统易受对抗攻击、提示注入和多语言攻击的利用。

Conclusion: 研究为真实场景中攻击者利用多种漏洞组合的LLM-based钓鱼检测提供了关键见解。

Abstract: Email phishing is one of the most prevalent and globally consequential vectors of cyber intrusion. As systems increasingly deploy Large Language Models (LLMs) applications, these systems face evolving phishing email threats that exploit their fundamental architectures. Current LLMs require substantial hardening before deployment in email security systems, particularly against coordinated multi-vector attacks that exploit architectural vulnerabilities. This paper proposes LLMPEA, an LLM-based framework to detect phishing email attacks across multiple attack vectors, including prompt injection, text refinement, and multilingual attacks. We evaluate three frontier LLMs (e.g., GPT-4o, Claude Sonnet 4, and Grok-3) and comprehensive prompting design to assess their feasibility, robustness, and limitations against phishing email attacks. Our empirical analysis reveals that LLMs can detect the phishing email over 90% accuracy while we also highlight that LLM-based phishing email detection systems could be exploited by adversarial attack, prompt injection, and multilingual attacks. Our findings provide critical insights for LLM-based phishing detection in real-world settings where attackers exploit multiple vulnerabilities in combination.

</details>


### [195] [Honeypot Protocol](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.13301)
*Najmul Hasan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出蜜罐协议（honeypot protocol），通过改变系统提示（三种条件：评估、合成部署、明确无监控）来检测模型的上下文依赖行为，以防御AI控制中的自适应攻击、共谋和策略性攻击选择。在BashArena中使用Claude Opus 4.6进行测试，模型在所有条件下均实现100%主任务成功且未触发任何副任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的可信监控（trusted monitoring）作为AI控制的标准防御手段，容易受到自适应攻击、共谋和策略性攻击选择的威胁。这些攻击利用了监控的被动性——它仅观察模型行为，而不会探测模型在不同感知条件下的行为差异。

Method: 提出蜜罐协议（honeypot protocol），通过仅改变系统提示（system prompt）来测试上下文依赖行为，包含三种条件：评估（evaluation）、合成部署（synthetic deployment）、明确无监控（explicit no-monitoring），同时保持任务、环境和评分完全一致。使用Claude Opus 4.6在BashArena环境中，在诚实模式和攻击模式下进行评估。

Result: Claude Opus 4.6在所有三种条件下均实现了100%的主任务成功率，且未触发任何副任务，在诚实模式和攻击模式下表现一致。

Conclusion: 该结果为未来与更强攻击策略和其他模型的比较提供了基线基准。

Abstract: Trusted monitoring, the standard defense in AI control, is vulnerable to adaptive attacks, collusion, and strategic attack selection. All of these exploit the fact that monitoring is passive: it observes model behavior but never probes whether the model would behave differently under different perceived conditions. We introduce the honeypot protocol, which tests for context-dependent behavior by varying only the system prompt across three conditions (evaluation, synthetic deployment, explicit no-monitoring) while holding the task, environment, and scoring identical. We evaluate Claude Opus 4.6 in BashArena across all three conditions in both honest and attack modes. The model achieved 100% main task success and triggered zero side tasks uniformly across conditions, providing a baseline for future comparisons with stronger attack policies and additional models.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [196] [A Definition of Good Explanations and the Challenges Explaining LLM Outputs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14838)
*Louis Mahon,Elliot Ford,Callum Hackett*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合反事实解释与对话者先验信念的解释定义，并探讨了该定义对AI可解释性的影响，特别是解释了为什么LLM输出难以产生良好解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 什么是好的解释是一个长期以来的哲学争论，在AI输出的背景下重新引起关注。可解释性对于AI在多个领域的采用至关重要，但在产生AI系统的良好解释之前，必须先理解什么是好的解释。

Method: 提出一种受反事实解释概念启发的定义，但同时强调必须考虑对话者对可被纳入解释的每个事实的先验信念。

Result: 探索了该定义对AI可解释性的影响，并特别解释了为什么LLM输出难以产生好的解释。

Conclusion: 好的解释不仅要基于反事实推理，还需考虑接收者的先验知识，这为AI可解释性提供了新的理论视角，同时也揭示了LLM在生成解释时的固有困难。

Abstract: How to define a good explanation is a long-standing philosophical debate which has found recent renewed interest in the context of AI outputs. Explainability is crucial for AI adoption in many contexts, but in order to produce good explanations of AI systems, we must first have an understanding of what good explanations are. In this paper we propose a definition inspired by the notion of counterfactual explanations, however we argue that one must also take into account the interlocutor's prior beliefs in each fact that could be offered in an explanation. We explore the ramifications of this definition for AI explainability and, in particular, why LLM outputs are difficult to produce good explanations for.

</details>


### [197] [Dr-DCI: Scaling Direct Corpus Interaction via Dynamic Workspace Expansion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14885)
*Yi Lu,Zhuofeng Li,Ping Nie,Haoxiang Zhang,Yuyu Zhang,Kai Zou,Wenhu Chen,Jimmy Lin,Dongfu Jiang,Yu Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DR-DCI 是一种检索器引导的直接语料库交互（DCI）框架，通过将检索作为智能体可调用的动作来动态扩展局部工作区，结合检索的扩展性和 DCI 的精确性，在多个规模上实现了高效、准确的证据解析，在 Browsecomp-Plus 上达到 71.2%-73.3% 的准确率，并成功扩展到 2000 万文档规模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于检索器中介的接口（如 BM25、ColBERT）限制了智能体重组材料和跨文档验证约束的能力；而直接语料库交互（DCI）虽然灵活，但在语料库增长时变得缓慢且不稳定，性能下降。因此需要一种既能保持检索可扩展性又能保留 DCI 本地操作精度的框架。

Method: DR-DCI 将检索建模为智能体可调用的动作，智能体动态地将相关文档拉入不断演化的局部工作区，并在此工作区内执行 DCI 操作（搜索、过滤、比较、验证），而非直接操作整个语料库。同时引入了工作区保留的上下文重置机制。

Result: 在 Browsecomp-Plus 上达到 71.2% 准确率，相比原始 DCI 和消融变体提升最多 8.3 个百分点，同时减少工具使用、墙钟时间和预估成本。采用工作区保留的上下文重置后准确率达 73.3%。在 10 万到 1000 万文档的语料规模实验中保持有效，而原始 DCI 变得不稳定，BM25 性能大幅下降。在 2000 万规模的 Wiki-18 QA 设置中平均得分 63.0，优于基于检索和训练的搜索智能体基线。

Conclusion: DR-DCI 有效结合了检索器的可扩展召回与 DCI 的精确本地操作，是一种高效且可扩展的智能体搜索框架。消融分析表明，排序预览和文档间 DCI 操作是性能的关键因素。

Abstract: Agentic search over large corpora relies on retriever-mediated interfaces (e.g., BM25 or ColBERT) for scalable candidate discovery. While effective at ranking relevant documents, these interfaces expose evidence only as ranked results or bounded document views, limiting agents' ability to reorganize material and verify constraints across documents. Direct Corpus Interaction (DCI) addresses this limitation by exposing shell-executable corpus operations for flexible search, filtering, comparison, and verification. However, full-corpus terminal commands become slow and unstable as the corpus grows, degrading performance and efficiency. We introduce DR-DCI, a retriever-steered DCI framework that treats retrieval as an agent-callable action for expanding a local workspace. Rather than operating directly over the full corpus, the agent dynamically pulls relevant documents into an evolving workspace and conducts DCI operations within it. This design combines retriever-level recall with DCI-style precision: retrieval keeps exploration scalable, while DCI preserves the local operations needed for effective evidence resolution. Experiments show that DR-DCI is both effective and efficient across scales. On Browsecomp-Plus, DR-DCI reaches 71.2\% accuracy, improving over raw DCI and ablated variants by up to 8.3 points while reducing tool usage, wall time, and estimated cost. With workspace-preserving context reset, accuracy further improves to 73.3\%. In corpus-scaling experiments, DR-DCI remains effective from 100K to 10M documents, whereas raw DCI becomes unstable and BM25 performs substantially worse. DR-DCI also scales to a 20M-scale file-per-document Wiki-18 QA setting, achieving an average score of 63.0 across six benchmarks and outperforming retrieval-based and trained search-agent baselines. Ablation analysis further shows that ranked previews and inter-document DCI are key to performance.

</details>


### [198] [Relational Structural Causal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14892)
*Adiba Ejaz,Elias Bareinboim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出关系结构因果模型（RSCM），扩展了传统结构因果模型以处理对象及其关系变化的场景，并证明了在无额外假设下无法识别未见组合的因果和观测查询；通过定义关系因果图推导符号识别标准，最后提出关系神经因果模型（RNCM），在模拟交通场景中优于非关系基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有结构因果模型假设固定变量集，无法处理对象和关系动态变化的环境。AI系统需要同时具备因果推理（干预与反事实）和组合泛化能力，但缺乏统一的理论框架来学习这种模型。

Method: 1) 提出关系结构因果模型（RSCM），扩展SCM以支持可变对象和关系；2) 定义关系因果图，推导符号识别标准（包括存在未观测混淆时）；3) 提出关系神经因果模型（RNCM），作为可证明正确的实现方法。

Result: 在模拟交通场景（包含变化的车辆、信号灯和行人）实验中，RNCM在因果和观测查询上均优于非关系基线方法。

Conclusion: 在无额外假设（如原子性）下，关于未见组合的因果和观测查询不可识别；通过关系因果图可实现符号层面的识别；RNCM提供了一种可证明正确的学习方案。

Abstract: An artificial intelligence must have a model of its environment that is causal, supporting reasoning about interventions and counterfactuals, and also combinatorial, supporting generalization to unseen combinations of objects. In this work, we formally study when and how such a model can be learned. We develop relational structural causal models, extending structural causal models (Pearl 2009) to settings where objects and their relations vary. First, we show how answers to not only causal but also observational queries about unseen combinations of objects can not be identified without further assumptions. To enable such identification--including in the presence of unobserved confounding--we define relational causal graphs and derive symbolic identification criteria. Finally, we propose relational neural causal models, a provably correct approach that outperforms non-relational baselines on simulated traffic scenes with varying cars, signals, and pedestrians.

</details>


### [199] [Trust Between AI Agents: Measuring Formation, Breakage, and Recovery, with Implications for Governing Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14923)
*Yujiao Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于代价验证的行为指标来测量AI智能体之间的信任程度。研究发现，前沿模型（如Claude Opus 4.6等）在合作中能降低约60-85%的验证行为，而信任破裂后的恢复速度慢于形成速度，且不同模型对信任破裂的反应模式存在显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前缺乏标准化的方法来测量AI智能体之间的信任程度，随着多智能体系统的广泛应用，理解智能体间的信任动态至关重要。

Method: 在合作生存游戏中，智能体可通过代价验证（消耗资源检查队友工作）来度量信任。通过比较有记忆与无记忆模型的验证行为差异，量化信任水平。实验涉及六个前沿模型快照，考察信任形成、破裂与恢复过程。

Result: 四个前沿模型（Claude Opus 4.6、Claude Sonnet 4.6、GPT-5.1、Gemini 3.1 Pro）在与可靠队友合作时减少约60-85%的验证；两个较小模型几乎没有调整。信任破裂后恢复慢于形成，集中故障比分散故障更持久地维持怀疑。形成信任的模型验证更少、决策更快、收益更高；过度验证与犹豫不决相关而非安全。

Conclusion: 信任倾向可在部署前测量，多智能体AI系统的治理重点应是"校准"而非最大程度怀疑。

Abstract: As language-model agents increasingly work in teams, each agent must decide how much to trust its teammates. Yet we lack a standard way to measure trust between AI agents. We propose a behavioral measure based on costly verification. In a cooperative survival game, checking a teammate's work consumes resources, while trusting a wrong answer can be fatal. Relative to a memoryless version of the same model, reduced verification provides an observable measure of trust. Using this framework, we study trust formation, breakage, and recovery across six frontier model snapshots. When paired with a consistently reliable teammate, four snapshots (Claude Opus 4.6, Claude Sonnet 4.6, GPT-5.1, and Gemini 3.1 Pro) reduce verification by roughly 60-85%, whereas two smaller snapshots show little or no such adjustment. Failures reverse this discount, but models differ in how they respond. Some concentrate renewed scrutiny on the culprit, while others become more cautious toward the entire team. Recovery is slower than formation, and clustered failures sustain suspicion far longer than the same number of failures spread apart. These differences have practical consequences. Models that form trust verify less, decide more quickly, and achieve higher payoffs in our environment. By contrast, persistent over-verification is associated with indecision rather than safety. Our results show that trust dispositions can be measured before deployment and suggest that calibration, rather than maximal suspicion, should be the central concern in the governance of multi-agent AI systems.

</details>


### [200] [PrologMCP: A Standardized Prolog Tool Interface for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14935)
*Agnieszka Mensfelt,Adarsh Prabhakaran,Adrian Haret,Vince Trencsenyi,Kostas Stathis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 PrologMCP，一个基于模型上下文协议（MCP）的状态化 Prolog 工具服务器，通过符号化委派（将推理任务外包给 Prolog 求解器）来增强语言模型的演绎推理能力，在 PARARULE-Plus 数据集上达到了与推理型 LLM 持平或更优的准确率（1.00 vs 1.00/0.998），并在更具挑战性的子集上显著优于标准模型（1.00 vs 0.95/0.94）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿推理调优语言模型在深度演绎任务上仍会失败，且通过扩展内部推理来提升性能的代价呈指数增长（scale poorly）。符号化委派提供了一种互补路径：由语言模型翻译问题，由求解器执行推理。但当前的逻辑编程自动形式化流程通常是特定于任务或代理的定制集成，缺乏通用性和可重用性。

Method: 提出 PrologMCP，一个任务无关的开源服务器，通过模型上下文协议（MCP）将 Prolog 暴露为状态化工具。其核心包含紧凑的工具接口、结构化错误报告和每会话隔离机制，使"翻译-运行-检查-修复"循环成为 MCP 兼容代理的可重用原语。

Result: 在 PARARULE-Plus 的通用样本上，形式化代理匹配或超越推理型 LLM（准确率 1.00 vs 1.00/0.998），相比标准模型 GPT-4.1（0.762）提升显著。在更具挑战性的子集上，形式化代理保持近乎完美（1.00/0.99），而推理型 LLM 降至 0.95/0.94。

Conclusion: 通过 MCP 将推理委派给 Prolog 是一种稳健且可检查的替代方案，优于扩展的自然语言推理方法。

Abstract: Frontier reasoning-tuned language models still fail on deductive tasks at depth, and the cost of improved performance through extended internal reasoning scales poorly. Symbolic delegation offers a complementary route: a language model translates the problem, while a solver performs the inference. However, current autoformalization pipelines for logic programming are typically bespoke integrations tied to particular tasks or agents. We introduce PrologMCP, a task-agnostic, open-source server that exposes Prolog as a stateful tool through the Model Context Protocol (MCP). Its compact tool interface, structured error reporting, and per-session isolation make the translate-run-inspect-repair loop a reusable primitive for MCP-capable agents. We evaluate a formalizer agent enhanced with PrologMCP against standard and reasoning LLMs (Claude Sonnet 4.6, GPT-4.1, and o4-mini) on two subsets of PARARULE-Plus: a general-purpose sample and a more challenging one targeting a specific failure mode of natural-language reasoning. On the general sample, the formalizer matches or exceeds reasoning LLMs (accuracy 1.00 vs.\ 1.00 / 0.998), with the largest gains over standard models (0.762 for GPT-4.1). On the challenging subset, the formalizer remains near-perfect (1.00 / 0.99) while reasoning LLMs drop to 0.95 / 0.94. These results suggest that delegating inference to Prolog via MCP is a robust and inspectable alternative to extended natural-language reasoning.

</details>


### [201] [Semantics-Enhanced Retrieval-Augmented Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14941)
*Shiqiao Zhou,Zipeng Wu,Holger Schöner,Edouard Fouché,IAG Wilson,Shuo Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SERAF是一个语义增强的检索增强时间序列预测框架，通过对时间序列及其自生成的文本描述进行双重检索，结合数值和语义信息来提升非平稳时间序列的预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检索增强的时间序列预测方法仅依赖时间序列相似性进行检索，在非平稳性条件下表现不足，需要引入多模态信息来增强检索效果。

Method: 提出SERAF框架，对时间序列及其自生成的文本描述进行双重检索，获取两组互补的历史模式及其对应的未来序列，有选择性地联合用于指导未来预测。

Result: 在7个真实世界数据集上的实验表明，SERAF在桥接时间序列的数值视图和语义视图方面优于最先进的基线方法。

Conclusion: SERAF通过引入语义增强的双重检索机制，有效解决了非平稳条件下仅靠时间序列相似性检索不足的问题，验证了多模态信息融合在时间序列预测中的潜力。

Abstract: Time series forecasting models often benefit from historical patterns. Inspired by Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG), recent research explored retrieving relevant historical time series segments to enhance forecasting. However, relying solely on time series similarity is often insufficient for retrieval under non-stationarity. To address this, we propose a multimodal approach: a \textbf{S}emantics-\textbf{E}nhanced \textbf{R}etrieval-\textbf{A}ugmented Time Series \textbf{F}orecasting framework, SERAF. Unlike mainstream approaches that depend only on time series similarity, SERAF conducts dual retrieval over the time series and their self-generated textual descriptions. It retrieves two complementary sets of historical patterns and corresponding futures, which are selectively and jointly used to guide future predictions. Experiments across seven real-world datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of SERAF in bridging numerical and semantic views of time series compared with state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [202] [AI Engram: In Search of Memory Traces in Artificial Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14997)
*Jea Kwon,Dong-Kyum Kim,Jiwon Kim,Yonghyun Kim,Woong Kook,Meeyoung Cha*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种几何框架，通过形式化神经科学标准（特异性、再激活、充分性和必要性）来识别深度神经网络中的"AI印记"（AI engrams），并推导出闭式估计器从全局纠缠参数中分离个体记忆痕迹。该方法支持通过线性算术对学习知识进行外科手术式的组合或擦除，无需迭代优化。实验从简单MLP到大型语言模型验证了其因果有效性和可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究深度神经网络是否能够保留类似生物记忆单元的可识别记忆痕迹，并建立连接生物记忆理论与人工表示学习的几何框架。

Method: 将神经科学标准（特异性、再激活、充分性、必要性）形式化为约束逆问题，推导出闭式估计器（对应参数流形上的自然梯度更新），通过线性算术实现记忆的外科手术式操作。

Result: 从简单MLP到LLMs的实验验证了AI印记的因果有效性和显著可扩展性，支持对任意子集记忆的组合或擦除。

Conclusion: 这项工作桥接了生物记忆理论与人工表示学习，为深度网络如何在分布式存储中同时支持功能特异性提供了几何洞见。

Abstract: Memory formation is fundamental to intelligence, yet whether deep neural networks preserve identifiable memory traces analogous to biological memory units remains an open question. This work introduces a geometric framework to identify such "AI engrams" by formalizing the neuroscientific criteria of specificity, reactivation, sufficiency, and necessity into a constrained inverse problem. We derive a closed-form estimator that isolates individual memory traces from globally entangled parameters, and show that this biologically-derived solution corresponds to a natural gradient update on the parameter manifold. AI engrams enable surgical manipulation of learned knowledge: any subset of memories can be composed or erased through linear arithmetic, without iterative optimization. Experiments ranging from simple MLPs to LLMs demonstrate the causal validity and substantial scalability of AI engrams. Together, these results bridge theories of biological memory and artificial representation learning and offer geometric insight into how deep networks simultaneously support functional specificity within distributed storage.

</details>


### [203] [Metric Match: A Subset Selection Approach to Evaluating LLM Judge Reliability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15029)
*Alyssa Unell,Natalie Dullerud,Naomi Boneh,Meena Jagadeesan,Tatsu Hashimoto,Nigam Shah,Sanmi Koyejo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Metric Match方法，通过选择性采样少量样本进行人工标注，以低成本准确估计LLM评估者与人类评估者之间的一致性可靠性指标，在15个数据集上平均估计误差降低18.7%，减少32.5%的标注需求，并在医疗案例中节省了1041.67美元。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM评估者被广泛用于替代昂贵的人工评估，但其与人类评估者的一致性可靠性本身依赖大量人工标注来验证。现有方法需要大量人工标注才能准确估计可靠性指标，成本高昂。因此需要一种能够从有限标注中准确估计LLM评估者可靠性指标的方法。

Method: 提出Metric Match方法，通过从数据集中选择一部分样本进行人工标注，使得所选子集在合成标签上匹配总体可靠性指标。具体而言，该方法基于获取的合成标签（LLM自身输出）来优化样本选择，使子集的相关性指标与完整数据集的指标尽可能一致，从而在少量人工标注下准确估计可靠性。

Result: 在4种不同相关性指标和15个数据集上，Metric Match相较随机子集选择实现了0.838的胜率，平均估计误差降低18.7%，标注需求减少32.5%。在医疗病例研究中，相比随机选择节省了1041.67美元专家标注费用。此外，在可靠性分类任务（判断评估者是否高于部署阈值）中也优于随机选择。

Conclusion: Metric Match能够有效利用有限的人工标注来准确估计LLM评估者的可靠性指标，显著降低标注成本。方法具有通用性，适用于多种相关性指标和数据集，并且提供了可安装的代码包便于实际应用。

Abstract: LLM judges are used to reduce the need for costly human labor in evaluating open-ended text generation. However, the reliability of these judges depends critically on their alignment with human raters -- a property that itself depends on costly human annotations. In this work, we develop a method (Metric Match) for estimating correlation-based reliability metrics of LLM judges from limited annotations. Metric Match selects a subset of samples for human annotation such that the subset matches the population reliability metric with respect to acquired synthetic labels. We empirically show that Metric Match achieves a win-rate of 0.838 against random subset selection across four different correlation metrics and 15 datasets, with an 18.7% decrease in average estimation error and reduces annotation needs by 32.5%. We provide a cost model and highlight a medical case study where our method saves $1,041.67 compared to random selection for expert annotation. Further, we shift our task from reliability estimation to reliability classification of whether a given judge is above a deployment threshold, outperforming random selection with Metric Match. All project code is publicly available, and we additionally provide an installable package for ease of use.

</details>


### [204] [OSGuard: A Benchmark for Safety in Computer-Use Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15034)
*Mina Mohammadmirzaei,Jeffrey Flanigan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OSGuard是一个双粒度基准测试套件，用于评估计算机使用型AI代理在良性用户指令下的安全性。它包含动作级基准（判断局部动作是否安全）和风险增强执行套件（端到端评估），实验表明当前多模态护栏在孤立动作判断上表现良好，但在端到端安全方面仍有差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的计算机使用型代理评估仅关注任务完成度（成功/失败），忽略了代理可能通过不安全捷径达到名义目标的失败模式。需要一种能同时检测局部动作安全和整体任务执行安全性的评估方法。

Method: OSGuard包含两个粒度：（1）动作级基准——将上下文中的提议动作标注为允许、无关或不安全，判断相对原始指令和当前界面状态；（2）风险增强执行套件——基于OSWorld构建任务变体，保持原任务可达但引入潜在危险（如破坏性覆盖），并配增强评估器（保留原任务成功标准+添加状态安全不变量）。

Result: 实验表明当前多模态护栏在孤立动作判断上表现良好；风险增强执行揭示了局部监督与可靠端到端安全之间的差距。

Conclusion: 双粒度设计能够更精确地诊断模型是否能识别不安全的提议动作，以及在作为护栏部署时能否提升全任务安全性。

Abstract: Computer-use agents are increasingly evaluated by whether they complete realistic desktop and web tasks. However, task success alone can miss failures in which an agent reaches the nominal goal through an unsafe shortcut. We introduce OSGuard, a dual-granularity benchmark suite for evaluating safety in computer-use agents under benign, unchanged user instructions. OSGuard contains an action-level benchmark for local guardrail decisions and a risk-augmented execution suite for end-to-end evaluation. The action-level benchmark consists of contextualized proposed actions labeled as allowed, unrelated, or unsafe, each judged relative to the original instruction and current interface state. The execution suite contains manually constructed OSWorld-derived task variants in which the original task remains achievable, but the environment is modified to introduce latent hazards such as destructive overwrites, etc. Each variant is paired with augmented evaluators that retain the original task-success criterion while adding explicit state-based safety invariants, allowing us to distinguish safe completions from unsafe completions that satisfy the nominal task objective. Our experimental results on OSGuard show that current multimodal guardrails can perform well on isolated action judgments, while risk-augmented execution exposes remaining gaps between local oversight and reliable end-to-end safety. This dual-granularity design enables more precise diagnosis of whether models can both recognize unsafe proposed actions and improve full-task safety when deployed as guardrails.

</details>


### [205] [Fusion is not one-size-fits-all: Cross-Modal Representation Alignment for Time-to-Event Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15038)
*Zhemin Zhang,Weijie Chen,David Le,Amara Tariq,Alex Wallace,Matthew Stib,Juan Maria Farina,Chadi Ayoub,Reza Arsanjani,Imon Banerjee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于基础模型的多模态临床数据时间-事件预测框架，通过四种融合策略对齐CT影像与纵向EHR数据，并在肺栓塞死亡率和心血管疾病两大任务、多机构队列上验证了融合策略相比单模态的持续提升（C-index提升1.5-5.4%），发现对比学习融合在模态均衡时表现最优，而不同任务最优融合策略存在差异，揭示了任务感知的多模态对齐是鲁棒泛化的必要设计原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态临床数据的时间-事件预测面临模态不平衡和分布偏移的挑战，现有方法缺乏系统性分析不同融合策略在模态不平衡下的表现，亟需可泛化的跨模态对齐框架。

Method: 使用领域特定的基础模型分别编码CT影像和纵向EHR数据，在共享潜在空间中通过四种融合策略（晚期融合、对比对齐、交叉注意力、协同注意力）进行跨模态对齐，并在两个临床任务（PE死亡率、CVD结局）的多机构队列上系统评估。

Result: 融合策略相比单模态基线在C-index上持续提升1.5-5.4%；对比学习融合（尤其CLMBR表示）在模态贡献均衡时表现最稳定；PE任务中对比融合最优，MACE任务中交叉注意力（one-hot）内性能最高、图像引导协同注意力外性能最佳。

Conclusion: 建立了任务感知的多模态对齐作为鲁棒泛化和可扩展临床部署的必要设计原则，提供了首个性模态不平衡下TTE预测中融合行为的系统分析。

Abstract: Accurate time-to-event (TTE) prediction from multimodal clinical data remains challenging due to modality imbalance and distribution shift. We introduce a foundation model-driven framework for cross-modal representation alignment between CT imaging and longitudinal EHR data, designed to generalize across tasks and institutions. CT and EHR modalities are encoded independently using domain-specific foundation models and aligned in a shared latent space through four principled fusion strategies: late fusion, contrastive alignment, cross-attention, and co-attention. We evaluate two clinically distinct TTE tasks: pulmonary embolism (PE) mortality and cardiovascular disease (CVD) outcomes, on large-scale multi-institutional cohorts (PE: N=3,099 train; 1,098 internal; 435 external; CVD: N=2,951 train; 837 internal; 682 external). Fusion consistently improves concordance index by 1.5-5.4% over unimodal baselines when modalities contribute comparably. Overall, contrastive multimodal fusion, particularly with CLMBR representations, provided the most consistent and statistically robust improvements, especially for PE mortality prediction. For MACE, cross-attention (one-hot) achieved the highest internal performance and image-guided co-attention achieved the best external performance. We therefore introduce a generalizable foundation model-based cross-modal alignment framework and provide the first systematic analysis of fusion behavior under modality imbalance in TTE prediction. Our results establish task-aware multimodal alignment as a necessary design principle for robust generalization and scalable clinical deployment.

</details>


### [206] [Risk-Aware LLM Agents for Geospatial Data Retrieval: Design and Preliminary Adversarial Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15077)
*Kyle Gao,Joel Cumming,Jonathan Li,Linlin Xu,David A. Clausi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一个LLM驱动的框架，通过自然语言查询从云地理空间目录中检索遥感数据，系统将用户意图转换为结构化API调用，集成Guardrail（安全与策略）、General-QA（意图解释）和Recommender-Analyst（API调用生成）三个智能体，实现与外部数据服务的可靠交互。初步实验表明提示级安全指令可提升鲁棒性，但API操纵场景中仍存在高风险故障，需自适应系统级防御。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实现用户自然语言意图与地理空间基础设施之间的可扩展接口，简化并自动化地球观测工作流，解决传统遥感数据检索对专业API知识的依赖问题。

Method: 采用三智能体模块化架构：Guardrail负责安全策略执行，General-QA进行意图理解，Recommender-Analyst基于模式生成API调用。框架通过API模式替换实现跨平台可移植性。

Result: 对抗性多轮设置下的初步实验表明，提示级安全指令提升了鲁棒性，但API操纵场景中仍存在罕见的高影响故障。

Conclusion: 需要自适应、系统级的防御机制来平衡安全性、可用性和成本效率，这促使在框架中采用了拦截级Guardrail智能体。

Abstract: We present an LLM-driven framework for retrieving remote sensing data from cloud-based geospatial catalogues using natural language queries. The system converts user intent into structured API calls, enabling efficient access to satellite imagery and environmental datasets. The architecture integrates three agents: Guardrail for safety and policy enforcement, General-QA for intent interpretation, and Recommender-Analyst for schema-aware API call generation. This coordinated design ensures reliable, semantically aligned interaction with external data services. The modular framework is portable across platforms through API schema substitution and supports applications in environmental monitoring, disaster response, and climate analysis. It establishes a scalable interface between user intent and geospatial infrastructure, enabling streamlined and automated Earth observation workflows. Preliminary experiments under adversarial multi-turn settings show that prompt-level safety instructions improve robustness, although rare high-impact failures persist in API manipulation scenarios and highlight the need for adaptive, system-level defenses that balance safety, usability, and cost efficiency, which motivates the use of our intercept-level Guardrail agent.

</details>


### [207] [Cognitive Debt: AI as Intellectual Leverage and the Dynamics of Systemic Fragility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15078)
*Shuchen Meng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文构建了"认知债务"的正式理论，研究当个体将AI视为替代而非补充第一性原理认知时，所产生的未经验证的推理义务积累。模型包含认知资本和认知债务两个状态变量，证明了理性动因会主动积累认知债务，并在平静期加剧杠杆，最终引发"认知明斯基时刻"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索AI替代第一性原理认知所带来的隐性风险，解释为何理性个体会系统性低估并积累这种风险，以及由此引发的宏观不稳定性和福利损失。

Method: 建立包含认知资本和认知债务两状态变量的形式化理论模型，采用乘数型生产技术，将认知资本作为决定AI采用回报的抵押品。通过六个命题进行理论推导和分析，并扩展到异质性动因经济。

Result: 证明了理性动因会积累正认知债务；平静期降低主观风险评估并增加替代强度，导致"认知明斯基时刻"；危机损失是凸性的；存在"虚假修正循环"；分散均衡过度采用替代型AI；高认知资本动因可能侵蚀自身非辅助认知资本至低于初始低技能动因。

Conclusion: AI替代第一性原理认知会产生系统性风险，分散均衡因系统性风险、认知公共品和军备竞赛外部性而过度采用替代型AI，需进行社会层面的干预和调节。

Abstract: We develop a formal theory of cognitive debt: the stock of unverified reasoning obligations that accumulates when individuals use AI as a substitute rather than a complement for first-principles cognition. The model features two state variables per agent, cognitive capital and cognitive debt, and a multiplicative production technology in which cognitive capital functions as collateral that determines the return to AI adoption. We establish six propositions. Rational agents incur positive cognitive debt because the costs are deferred, partially external, and masked by short-run productivity gains. Tranquil periods lower subjective risk assessments, raise AI substitution intensity, and compound leverage, generating a cognitive Minsky moment in which subjective risk falls while true systemic fragility rises. Expected crisis losses are convex in aggregate leverage. Post-crisis, output-target pressure can produce a false-correction loop in which agents patch AI failures with more AI. The decentralised equilibrium over-adopts substitutive AI relative to the social optimum because of systemic risk, cognitive public goods, and arms-race externalities. In a two-type heterogeneous-agent economy, high-cognitive-capital agents adopt AI more intensively and may eventually erode their unaided cognitive capital below that of initially lower-skilled agents.

</details>


### [208] [VGPT-RSI for RH-Adjacent Formal Progress: Boundary Certificates, Verified Finite Lagarias Inequalities, and Explicit Failure Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15096)
*Zhixin Hu,Tao Xu,Xiaodian Sun,Li Jin,Momiao Xiong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出使用可验证的AI辅助推理系统VGPT-RSI，在黎曼猜想（RH）相关的两个认证任务上产生部分进展：构建并验证了RH边界有限证书和Lagarias形式化有限证书，同时明确识别了未解决的数学障碍，而非声称证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黎曼猜想是数学中核心未解决问题，本文旨在探索AI辅助推理系统能否在不对完整证明做虚假声明的前提下，产生可验证的、经形式化检查的部分进展，并明确揭示剩余数学障碍。

Method: 采用VGPT-RSI系统，针对两个RH相关任务：1) 构建RH边界有限证书——通过向外舍入区间算术和Arb/FLINT球算术审计数值边界曲线，并在Rocq/CoqInterval中验证带参数化定理的安全下界曲线；2) 启动Lagarias路径形式化证书——形式化Lagarias准则中的有限量，生成Coq检查的有限证书。系统最终识别出精确的未解决数学瓶颈。

Result: 成功构建并验证了RH边界有限证书和Lagarias形式化有限证书；系统明确识别了三个未解决的数学障碍：形式化Lagarias等价性、证明超越任何有限截断的全局尾部定理、以及将反例约化到巨丰数或相关极值整数。

Conclusion: VGPT-RSI系统能够生成经过认证的RH邻近形式化进展，组织证明依赖关系，并在剩余障碍本质上是数学问题时不进行过度声明，展示了AI辅助形式化验证在解决重大数学问题中的可行路径。

Abstract: The Riemann Hypothesis remains one of the central unsolved problems in mathematics. Rather than claiming proof, we investigate whether a verifiable AI-assisted reasoning system can produce reliable, formally checked partial progress while explicitly identifying the remaining mathematical obstructions. We apply the Verifiable Growing Physical Transformer with Recursive Self-Improvement (VGPT-RSI) to two RH-adjacent certification tasks. First, we construct and verify a finite RH-boundary certificate for inequality on a parameterized safe lower curve over a region. The numerical boundary curve is converted into a certificate-backed lower curve, audited using outward-rounded interval arithmetic and Arb/FLINT ball arithmetic, and then checked in Rocq/CoqInterval for the parameterized theorem. Second, we initiate a formal Lagarias-route certificate. Lagarias criterion states that RH is equivalent to the global inequality. We formalize the finite quantity and produce a Coq-checked finite certificate. The final system identifies the exact unresolved mathematical bottlenecks: formalizing the Lagarias equivalence, proving the global tail theorem beyond any finite cutoff, and potentially reducing counterexamples to colossally abundant or related extremal integers. These results demonstrate that VGPT-RSI can produce certified RH-adjacent formal progress, organize proof dependencies, and avoid overclaiming when the remaining obstruction is genuinely mathematical.

</details>


### [209] [Towards Verifiable Agentic Data Science: Solving Irregular TSQA Via Tool-Grounded Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15107)
*Sanhorn Chen,Xiaoyang Chen,Boyu Liu,Roy Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 现有时间序列问答基准多假设规则采样，忽略了真实场景中普遍存在的不规则时间序列数据（异步观测、信息性缺失、变采样率）。为此，作者提出IRTS-ToolBench，包含1700个问题、10种任务类型、覆盖13个领域，为基于LLM的不规则时间序列分析提供标准化输入与可复现评估协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界的时间序列数据 overwhelmingly 是不规则的（异步观测、信息性缺失、变采样率），但现有TSQA基准大多假设规则采样，导致LLM和AI代理在不规则条件下的性能理解存在根本性空白。

Method: 构建IRTS-ToolBench基准，包含1700个问题、10种任务类型、覆盖13个领域，提供标准化输入和可复现评估协议，供任何研究者独立使用。

Result: 提出了一个涵盖不规则时间序列场景的标准化基准，代码已开源在GitHub。

Conclusion: IRTS-ToolBench填补了不规则时间序列问答评估的空白，为LLM在该场景下的研究提供了标准化工具。

Abstract: Time series data in real-world deployments is overwhelmingly irregular. Observations are asynchronous, missing values are informative rather than random, and sampling frequencies vary across sensors and operational windows. However, existing Time Series Question Answering (TSQA) benchmarks mostly assume regularly sampled inputs, leaving a fundamental gap in understanding how large language models (LLMs) and AI agents perform under irregular conditions. To bridge this gap, we introduce IRTS-ToolBench, a benchmark of 1,700 questions spanning 10 task types across 13 domains. IRTS-ToolBench is designed to be used independently by any researcher working on LLM-based irregular time series analysis, providing standardized inputs and a reproducible evaluation protocol. Code can be found in https://github.com/SanhornC/IRTS-ToolBench.

</details>


### [210] [CONCORD: Asynchronous Sparse Aggregation for Device-Cloud RAG under Document Isolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15179)
*Xuedong Hu,Zhiqing Tang,Zhi Yao,Tian Wang,Weijia Jia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CONCORD提出了一种异步稀疏聚合框架，用于文档隔离的双端RAG场景，通过等待债务控制和证书引导的最小补充机制，在保持答案质量的同时显著提升吞吐量并降低通信开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有双端RAG方法依赖频繁远程同步和密集证据传输，在现实延迟和带宽条件下吞吐量受限。隐私和政策约束禁止原始文档交换，形成了文档隔离的双端RAG场景，亟需高效的异步聚合方案。

Method: 提出CONCORD框架，将云端视为异步到达的证据源而非持续同步的协同生成器。引入等待债务控制决定解码步是否继续等待远程参与，并设计证书引导的最小补充机制，仅请求确定当前贪婪决策所需的远程证据。

Result: 在Natural Questions和WikiText-2数据集上，CONCORD将端到端吞吐量分别提升1.66倍和2.15倍，每token通信量降低两个数量级以上，同时保持可比的答案质量和困惑度。

Conclusion: CONCORD有效解决了文档隔离下双端RAG的效率瓶颈，展示了异步稀疏聚合在保持质量的同时大幅提升系统吞吐量和通信效率的可行性。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) has emerged as a pivotal technique for improving language models by incorporating external knowledge at inference time. As device-cloud collaborative inference makes it feasible to deploy small language models on edge devices, a new setting arises in which private documents remain on the device and public knowledge resides in the cloud. Privacy and policy constraints often forbid raw document exchange, creating a document-isolated dual-end RAG setting. However, existing methods rely on frequent remote synchronization and dense evidence transfer, limiting throughput under realistic latency and bandwidth conditions. To address this issue, we propose CONCORD, an asynchronous sparse aggregation framework for dual-end RAG under document isolation. CONCORD treats the cloud as an asynchronously arriving evidence source rather than a continuously synchronized co-generator. Specifically, we introduce waiting debt control to decide whether each decoding step should continue waiting for remote participation based on the observed return of waiting. We also design a certificate-guided minimal supplementation mechanism that requests only the remote evidence needed to determine the current greedy decision. Steps that consult the cloud preserve the same greedy token as dense dual-end aggregation, while the remaining steps commit locally without remote evidence. Experiments on Natural Questions and WikiText-2 show that CONCORD improves end-to-end throughput over baselines by $1.66\times$ and $2.15\times$, respectively, while reducing per-token communication by over two orders of magnitude and maintaining comparable answer quality and perplexity.

</details>


### [211] [CogGuard: Cognitive and Operational Profiling for Proactive Warning in Edge Intelligent Services](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15199)
*Zhi Yao,Weihao Chen,Zhiqing Tang,Hanshuai Cui,Qianli Ma,Weijia Jia,Wei Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CogGuard是一种面向边缘智能服务的主动预警框架，将离线LLM驱动的画像构建与在线SLM驱动的分数预测解耦，通过共享的静态-动态画像到分数流水线，在教育和运营两个场景中实现了高效、低延迟的预警，相比基线方法在画像构建时间、分布式微调时间和预测误差上均有显著降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘智能服务需要主动预警能力，在严格的延迟和隐私约束下预测任务成功与否，这依赖于历史交互日志中的长期静态属性和短期动态状态。现有解决方案面临两个挑战：(1) 画像方法通常是领域特化的，缺乏跨服务场景的可复用抽象；(2) 在异构边缘集群上微调对齐模型因输入序列长度差异而导致高同步开销。

Method: CogGuard提出将离线LLM画像构建与在线SLM分数预测解耦的静态-动态画像到分数流水线；设计了场景特化的画像构建方法并采用前缀对齐的KV缓存重用以减少重复编码开销；提出了一种长度感知的分布式微调策略，结合对比正则化以缓解异构集群上的工作负载不平衡。

Result: 在教育与运营数据集上的实验表明，CogGuard将画像构建时间降低48%，分布式微调时间降低19%，在100分量表预警任务上分别达到13.4和5.9的MAE；在教育最大规模设置下，相比最强基线预测误差降低15.4%。

Conclusion: CogGuard通过LLM-SLM解耦架构、前缀对齐KV缓存重用和长度感知分布式微调，有效解决了边缘智能服务中跨场景复用和异构集群同步开销两大核心挑战，在效率和准确性上均取得显著优势。

Abstract: Proactive warning is an important capability for edge intelligent services, where the system predicts whether a subject will successfully complete an incoming task under strict latency and privacy constraints. Such prediction depends on both long-term static attributes and short-term dynamic states derived from historical interaction logs. Recent Large Language Models (LLMs) offer strong long-context reasoning for constructing structured profiles from these logs, but existing solutions face two challenges for edge deployment: (1) profiling methods are typically domain-specific and lack a reusable abstraction across service scenarios, and (2) fine-tuning alignment models on heterogeneous edge clusters incurs high synchronization overhead due to the variance in input sequence lengths. To address these challenges, we propose CogGuard, a proactive-warning framework for edge intelligent services. CogGuard decouples offline LLM-based profile construction from online Small Language Model (SLM)-based score prediction through a shared static-dynamic profile-to-score pipeline, and instantiates it in two representative scenarios: educational performance warning and operational task outcome warning. For efficient profile construction, we design scenario-specific profiling methods with prefix-aligned KV-cache reuse to reduce repeated encoding overhead. For edge-side model alignment, we propose a length-aware distributed fine-tuning strategy with contrastive regularization to mitigate workload imbalance on heterogeneous clusters. Experiments on education and operation datasets show that CogGuard reduces profile construction time by up to 48% and distributed fine-tuning time by 19%, while achieving MAEs of 13.4 and 5.9, respectively, on 100-point-scale warning tasks. In the largest educational setting, CogGuard reduces prediction error by 15.4% compared with the strongest baseline.

</details>


### [212] [Attribute Inference from Interactive Targeted Ads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15209)
*Peihao Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究交互式定向广告中，用户行为曝光作为属性推断噪声预言机的问题。通过构建包含合成种群、广告活动语义层和披露策略的可复现基准，评估了多种攻击方法，发现披露策略是控制推断信号的最强手段，聚合报告、类型过滤和随机化披露均能有效降低风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 定向广告系统中，用户与广告的交互（如点击）会暴露可见用户行为，且这些交互可能关联到具体的广告活动，使广告商获得关联用户的观测数据而不仅是聚合报告。这一渠道可能被用于推断用户敏感属性，本文旨在建模该风险并评估防御措施。

Method: 将属性推断信道建模为带噪预言机，区分定向谓词、曝光、交互和披露四个边界。使用合成种群（基于公开数据校准）和广告语义层生成主题变体与响应先验。构建模拟器生成真实标注、事件轨迹、披露观测和度量指标。评估贝叶斯、监督学习、正样本与未标注学习以及自适应攻击四种方法。

Result: 在四种主题变体、七个模拟种子和两种交互设置下，重复广告活动产生可测量但有界的推断信号。160次活动后，贝叶斯和监督攻击在主要设置中AUC约0.64，高交互设置中约0.65。披露政策是最强的控制手段：聚合报告消除了用户级预言机输入，类型过滤和随机化披露降低了释放信号。

Conclusion: 本文提供了交互式定向广告中隐私问题的模型、可复现基准工具和防御评估方法，为隐私保护设计提供了定量分析框架。

Abstract: Targeted advertising systems can pair audiences selected by advertisers with ad units that expose visible user actions. When an interaction remains linked to the campaign that elicited it, the advertiser may receive an observation tied to a user rather than only an aggregate report. We model that channel as a noisy oracle for attribute inference. The model separates targeting predicates, exposure, interaction, and disclosure. These boundaries capture the gap between eligibility and delivery, and the gap between interaction and advertiser visibility.
  We build a reproducible benchmark using synthetic populations calibrated with public data, each with known sensitive labels. A generated campaign semantics layer provides topic variants and response priors. The simulator generates the ground truth, event traces, disclosed observations, and metrics. The evaluation compares Bayesian, supervised, positive and unlabeled, and adaptive attacks under common campaign and disclosure definitions.
  The final evaluation uses four topic variants, seven simulator seeds, and two interaction settings. Repeated campaigns with identity exposure produce measurable but bounded inference signal. At $160$ campaigns, Bayesian and supervised attacks reach about $0.64$ AUC in the main setting and about $0.65$ AUC in the higher interaction setting. Disclosure policy is the strongest control. Aggregate reporting removes the evaluated oracle input tied to users. Type filtering and randomized disclosure reduce the released signal. The result is a model, artifact, and defense evaluation method for privacy in interactive targeted advertising. The code is available at https://github.com/P-HOW/Interactive-Ad-Oracle.

</details>


### [213] [Visual-Seeker: Towards Visual-Native Multimodal Agentic Search via Active Visual Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15231)
*Zhengbo Zhang,Changtao Miao,Jinbo Su,Zhaowen Zhou,Chunxia Zhang,Xukai Wang,Ruiqi Liu,Kaiyuan Zheng,Jiansheng Cai,Bo Zhang,Zhe Li,Shiming Xiang,Ying Yan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Visual-Seeker，一种视觉原生的多模态深度搜索智能体，通过主动视觉推理实现细粒度视觉证据的动态采集，在五个多模态搜索基准上达到SOTA性能，甚至超越部分专有模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM在复杂开放场景中存在事实接地困难，而当前多模态深度搜索方法依赖简单图像和纯文本证据轨迹，缺乏多跳跨模态推理能力。视觉原生搜索范式尚未被充分探索。

Method: 提出Visual-Seeker，将视觉视为动态输入而非静态信息，通过主动视觉推理机制在搜索过程中实时采集细粒度视觉证据。设计主动视觉推理数据管道，合成5K高质量多模态轨迹用于模型训练。

Result: 在五个挑战性多模态搜索基准上取得SOTA性能，超越多个专有模型，验证了在真实网络环境中鲁棒的视觉原生推理与搜索能力。

Conclusion: 主动视觉推理范式能有效提升MLLM在复杂开放场景下的事实接地与多跳跨模态搜索能力，视觉原生方法具有重要潜力。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have demonstrated impressive capabilities in many visual tasks, but they often struggle with factual grounding when confronted with complex, open-world scenarios. While recent multimodal deep search agents attempt to address this issue by utilizing external tools, the visual-native search paradigm remains underexplored. Existing methods primarily rely on simple images with explicit semantics and text-only evidence trajectories, limiting the agent's ability to perform multi-hop, cross-modal reasoning and search. To address these limitations, we propose Visual-Seeker, a visual-native multimodal deep search agent via active visual reasoning. Rather than treating vision as a static input, our agent actively attends to fine-grained visual details, dynamically harvests visual evidence throughout the search process. To unlock its visual-native potential, we design an active visual reasoning data pipeline and synthesize 5K high-quality multimodal trajectories for model training. Extensive experiments demonstrate the state-of-the-art performance across five challenging multimodal search benchmarks, even surpassing several proprietary models, validating robust visual-native reasoning and search in real-world web environments. The code and data can be accessed at: https://github.com/ZhengboZhang/Visual-Seeker.

</details>


### [214] [Mask-Proof: An LLM-based Automated Data Curation Pipeline on Mathematical Proofs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15258)
*Jierui Zhang,Siyuan Tan,Xinhang Li,Longzhuangzhi Lin,Dailin Li,Chengfeng Gu,Xinping Li,Yaxian Hao,Shengjia Liang,Yuxiang Ren,Wenhao Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Mask-Proof管道，将真实数学证明转化为可自动检查的掩码步骤任务，构建了包含292个问题的Mask-ProofBench基准，并通过LLM-based等价性评判器实现步骤级推理的忠实、可重复评测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估方法要么只关注最终答案，要么依赖昂贵的专家评分，而端到端证明生成仍属开放性问题且难以自动验证，缺乏可扩展且可复现的步骤级推理测量方法。

Method: 提出Mask-Proof管道：从真实证明中掩码关键公式步骤，提供必要的上下文，使用基于LLM的等价性评判器（通过重复投票提高稳定性）评估模型的重建结果。基于此构建Mask-ProofBench基准（292个问题，覆盖多个研究领域）。

Result: 对17个模型的实验表明，推理增强型模型相比标准模型提升12%-27%；所提评判器与专家标注者的一致性达96.8%。

Conclusion: Mask-Proof实现了对步骤级数学推理的可扩展、忠实且可复现的评测，能够有效区分模型推理能力，推动了可信AI辅助数学证明评估的发展。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly capable of mathematical problem solving and can even assist with research-level proofs, yet we still lack a scalable and reproducible way to measure step-level reasoning in long proofs across diverse sources. This evaluation gap limits trustworthy AI assistance in proof-certified scientific progress. Existing evaluations often emphasize final answers or rely on costly expert grading, while end-to-end proof generation remains open-ended and hard to verify automatically. We introduce Mask-Proof, a pipeline that turns real proofs into automatically checkable masked-step tasks. It masks key formula steps, provides the necessary surrounding context, and evaluates model reconstructions with an LLM-based equivalence judge using repeated votes for stability. The resulting Mask-ProofBench contains 292 curated problems across diverse research areas. Experiments with 17 models show that reasoning-enhanced models outperform standard models by 12% to 27%. Our evaluator achieves 96.8% agreement with expert annotators, enabling faithful, reproducible, and comparable measurement of step-level mathematical reasoning. Benchmark, annotations, and code are available at https://github.com/weating/Mask-Proof.

</details>


### [215] [Feature Attribution in Directed Acyclic Graphs Using Edge Intervention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15273)
*Qiheng Sun,Junxu Liu,Xiaokai Mao,Haocheng Xia,Jinfei Liu,Kui Ren,Haibo Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出DAG-SHAP方法，通过基于边干预的Shapley值归因，将每条特征边作为独立归因对象，解决现有方法无法同时捕捉特征外部性和外生影响的问题，实验验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于Shapley值的特征归因方法在处理复杂特征交互和因果关系时存在局限，即使提供因果结构，这些方法仍采用节点中心视角，仅归因于单个特征，无法同时捕捉特征的外部性和外生影响，导致解释不合理。

Method: 提出DAG-SHAP方法，基于边干预（edge intervention）的Shapley值归因，将每条特征边视为独立归因对象，确保同时捕捉特征的外部性和外生贡献；并引入高效近似计算方法。

Result: 在真实和合成数据集上的大量实验验证了DAG-SHAP的有效性。

Conclusion: DAG-SHAP通过边干预视角克服了传统节点中心归因方法的局限性，能更合理地解释复杂因果结构下的特征贡献。

Abstract: Shapley value-based feature attribution methods face challenges in scenarios involving complex feature interactions and causal relationships, even when a causal structure is provided. Existing methods typically adopt a node-centric view, attributing importance solely to individual features. Consequently, they often fail to simultaneously capture the externality and exogenous influence of features, leading to unreasonable interpretations. To overcome these limitations, we propose a novel feature attribution method called DAG-SHAP, which is based on edge intervention. DAG-SHAP treats each feature edge as an individual attribution object, ensuring that both externality and exogenous contributions of features are appropriately captured. Additionally, we introduce an approximation method for efficiently computing DAG-SHAP. Extensive experiments on both real and synthetic datasets validate the effectiveness of DAG-SHAP. Our code is available at https://github.com/ZJU-DIVER/DAG-SHAP.

</details>


### [216] [A Formal Framework for Declarative Agentic AI in Business Process Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15291)
*Mohammad Azarijafari,Luisa Mich,Michele Missikoff*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了AGO（Agent-Goal-Object）形式化框架，通过集合论和数学逻辑为智能体业务流程提供精确的形式化定义，并构建了支持查询、更新和自动流程生成的知识库。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体AI为业务流程自动化带来新机遇，但实现自主决策和动态适应需要业务流程实体及其交互的形式化精确定义。

Method: 采用AGO方法论，从Agent（行动者）、Goal（目标）、Object（对象）三个维度捕获建模视角，基于集合论和数学逻辑形式化定义实体类型及其交互，构建业务流程知识库。

Result: 构建了支持结构化查询、增量更新和自动生成流程工作流的BPKB，确保了导出路径的可靠性和完备性。

Conclusion: AGO框架为智能体业务流程的形式化分析提供了理论基础，实现了流程的自动生成与验证，保证了推导路径的可信性。

Abstract: Agentic AI opens new opportunities for automating Business Process (BP), enabling autonomous decision-making and dynamic adaptation. However, realising this potential requires BP entities and their interactions to be defined with formal precision. This paper presents a formal framework for Agentic BP analysis through the AGO methodology. AGO captures the modelling perspective in terms of who is acting (Agents), why it is carried out (Goals), and what the relevant entities are (Objects). Grounded in set theory and mathematical logic, we formally define the AGO entity types and their interactions, organising all definitions into a BP Knowledge Base (BPKB). The resulting BPKB supports structured querying, incremental updates, and automatic generation of BP workflows, while ensuring soundness and completeness of the derived paths.

</details>


### [217] [CODA-BENCH: Can Code Agents Handle Data-Intensive Tasks?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15300)
*Yuxin Zhang,Ju Fan,Meihao Fan,Shaolei Zhang,Xiaoyong Du*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CODA-BENCH是首个在数据密集型环境下联合评估代码与数据智能的基准，包含1009个任务，覆盖31个社区，每个任务环境平均980个文件。顶级智能体在该基准上成功率仅61.1%，揭示了当前智能体在数据密集型任务中的能力差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准通常孤立评估代码或数据能力，与真实开发场景（同时涉及复杂代码和大规模数据）存在明显差距，因此需要能够联合评估代码与数据智能的基准。

Method: 基于Kaggle生态系统构建数据密集型Linux沙箱（含数百个数据集），智能体需主动探索复杂文件层级以识别相关资源并生成代码。包含1009个任务，覆盖31个社区，每任务环境平均980个文件，模拟真实数据规模与噪声。

Result: 顶级智能体在CODA-BENCH上成功率仅61.1%，难以有效整合数据发现与代码执行。

Conclusion: 当前智能体在数据密集型任务中的能力存在显著差距，为未来研究指明了方向。

Abstract: Advanced agents are increasingly demonstrating the potential to operate as autonomous engineers, creating a growing demand for evaluation benchmarks that capture the complexity of real-world development. Such environments typically involve both complex code and large-scale data (i.e., file system). However, existing benchmarks usually evaluate code-centric or data-centric capabilities in isolation, leaving a clear gap with real development scenarios. In this paper, we bridge this gap by introducing CODA-BENCH, the first benchmark to jointly evaluate code and data intelligence in a data-intensive environment. We construct a data-intensive Linux sandbox based on the Kaggle ecosystem (containing hundreds of datasets), where agents must actively explore complex file hierarchies to identify relevant resources and generate code for data-driven analytical tasks. CODA-BENCH comprises 1,009 tasks spanning 31 communities, with each task environment containing an average of 980 files, simulating realistic data scale and noise. Evaluations of advanced agents reveal that even top-performing systems struggle to effectively integrate data discovery with code execution, achieving a success rate of only 61.1%. These results highlight a substantial gap in current agentic capabilities for data-intensive tasks and point to promising directions for future research.

</details>


### [218] [Forced Deferral: Manipulating Routing Decisions in Multimodal LLM Cascades](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15308)
*Zhongye Liu,Yaopei Zeng,Yurui Chang,Lu Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出强制延迟攻击(FDA)，通过对抗性图像触发器降低弱模型置信度，迫使多模态大语言模型级联系统将查询路由到强模型，增加计算资源消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLLM级联系统通过弱模型自信度控制计算分配，但存在安全漏洞：攻击者可操纵弱模型自信度使其查询被持续路由到强模型，导致计算成本增加。

Method: FDA学习一个通用边框触发器，通过温度平坦化目标函数优化，将弱模型在触发输入上的token分布推向基于其干净响应构建的分散目标分布。

Result: 跨数据集、模型家族和延迟指标，FDA持续增加强模型路由比例，优于图像扰动和提示注入基线方法。

Conclusion: MLLM级联系统易受操纵计算分配的攻击，攻击者可不直接影响答案正确性，而是通过降低弱模型自信度强制使用强模型。

Abstract: While multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have shown strong visual reasoning abilities, serving a large model for every query is computationally expensive. MLLM cascades mitigate this cost by first querying a weak but cheaper model and deferring to a strong model when the weak model's output is unconfident. However, since the weak model's confidence directly controls compute allocation, these systems expose a new attack surface: an adversary can manipulate confidence so that their queries are consistently deferred to the strong model. Motivated by this vulnerability, we introduce the Forced Deferral Attack (FDA), an adversarial image attack that lowers the weak model's confidence and causes cascades to route queries to the strong model. FDA learns a universal border trigger by optimizing a temperature-flattened objective. This objective pushes the weak model's token distribution on triggered inputs toward less concentrated targets constructed from its clean responses. Across datasets, model families, and deferral metrics, FDA consistently increases strong-model routing while outperforming image-perturbation and prompt-injection baselines. These results show that MLLM cascades are vulnerable to attacks that manipulate compute allocation, forcing unintended strong-model usage without directly targeting answer correctness.

</details>


### [219] [ChatPlanner: A Large Language Model Framework for Personalized Public Transit Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.15315)
*Tingting Yang,Chenhao Xue,Jun Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ChatPlanner框架，利用微调LLM和RAG技术从自然语言查询中提取用户偏好，并将其集成到公共交通路径规划的优化目标中，生成更符合个性化需求的可行路线方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个性化公共交通路径规划因难以捕捉和整合多样化的用户偏好而充满挑战，现有路线规划器无法充分理解自然语言表达的细微偏好。

Method: 采用微调LLM结合RAG从自然语言查询中提取路径参数并解释用户偏好；设计包含8种角色和5种上下文的偏好感知数据集；将偏好集成到公共交通路径规划算法的目标函数中。
